    12	// Snapshot rewrite throttle: journal append ALWAYS; the snapshot (and
    13	// HANDOFF.md) re-render only on an important event type, >30 s since the last
    14	// snapshot, or >=10 accreted events (plan §Checkpoint engine).
    15	const ROUTINE_TYPES = new Set(['note', 'file.touch']);
    16	const THROTTLE_WINDOW_MS = 30_000;
    17	const THROTTLE_EVENT_COUNT = 10;
    18	
    19	// Transcript tail bounds (plan §Transcript policy): last 10 messages, 8 KB cap,
    20	// opt-in via baton.config.json capture.transcriptTail, PreCompact only.
    21	const TAIL_MESSAGES = 10;
    22	const TAIL_BYTES = 8192;
    23	
    24	/**
    25	 * Opt-in transcript-tail capture (gate-2 fix 10). Returns the bounded,
    26	 * redacted tail or null when the gate is closed: config off (the default),
    27	 * not a PreCompact payload, no usable transcript_path, or an unreadable /
    28	 * symlinked file. The path comes from an untrusted hook payload — it is read
    29	 * only under explicit opt-in, must lstat as a regular file, and its content
    30	 * passes the secret-redaction filter before storage.
    31	 * @param {string} root @param {any} raw parsed hook payload @param {any} io
    32	 * @returns {string | null}
    33	 */
    34	function captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io) {
    35	  const cfg = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${root}/baton.config.json`);
    36	  if (!cfg.ok || cfg.value?.capture?.transcriptTail !== true) return null;
    37	
    38	  const eventName = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? (raw.hook_event_name ?? raw.event) : null;
    39	  if (typeof eventName !== 'string' || !/precompact/i.test(eventName)) return null;
    40	  const path = raw.transcript_path;
    41	  if (typeof path !== 'string' || path.length === 0) return null;
    42	
    43	  try {
    44	    if (!io.fs.lstatSync(path).isFile()) return null;
    45	    // Allowlist the transcript path (gate-2 iter-2 m1): the payload is
    46	    // untrusted, so its resolved realpath must sit under the repo root, the
    47	    // user's ~/.claude tree (where Claude Code actually writes transcripts),
    48	    // or a configured capture.transcriptDir — never an arbitrary readable file.
    49	    const fileReal = io.fs.realpathSync(path);
    50	    /** @type {string[]} */
    51	    const allowed = [root];
    52	    const home = io.env?.HOME;
    53	    if (typeof home === 'string' && home.length > 0) allowed.push(`${home}/.claude`);
    54	    const dir = cfg.value?.capture?.transcriptDir;
    55	    if (typeof dir === 'string' && dir.length > 0) allowed.push(dir);
    56	    const under = (/** @type {string} */ base) => {
    57	      let baseReal;
    58	      try {
    59	        baseReal = io.fs.realpathSync(base);
    60	      } catch {
    61	        baseReal = base;
    62	      }
    63	      // Compare on normalized forward-slash spellings (iter-3 F9): raw Windows
    64	      // backslash realpaths never satisfy a forward-slash containment, so
    65	      // opt-in capture would silently never fire.
    66	      return isContained(baseReal, fileReal);
    67	    };
    68	    if (!allowed.some(under)) return null;
    69	
    70	    const text = io.fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    71	    const lines = text.split('\n').filter((/** @type {string} */ l) => l.trim() !== '');
    72	    let tail = lines.slice(-TAIL_MESSAGES).join('\n');
    73	    if (Buffer.byteLength(tail) > TAIL_BYTES) {
    74	      tail = Buffer.from(tail).subarray(-TAIL_BYTES).toString('utf8');
    75	    }
    76	    return redactSecrets(tail);
    77	  } catch {
    78	    return null; // unreadable transcript is never a checkpoint failure
    79	  }
    80	}
    81	
    82	/**
    83	 * `baton checkpoint` — mechanical checkpoint from a hook payload on stdin.
    84	 * Hook-safety rule: exit 0 on every soft failure so a checkpoint can never
    85	 * break the host harness; `--strict` opts into hard failures (CI).
    86	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    87	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    88	 */
    89	export async function cmdCheckpoint(args, io) {
    90	  const { flags } = parseFlags(args);
    91	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
    92	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
    93	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
    94	
    95	  try {
    96	    return await run(flags, platform, io);
    97	  } catch (err) {
    98	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    99	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'checkpoint-failed', msg } });
   100	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${msg}\n`);
   101	    return strict ? 1 : 0;
   102	  }
   103	}
   104	
   105	/**
   106	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
   107	 * @param {string} platform
   108	 * @param {any} io
   109	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   110	 */
   111	async function run(flags, platform, io) {
   112	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
   113	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
   114	  const paths = bundlePaths(root);
   115	  // The --json contract (gate-2 fix 11): exactly one envelope on stdout for
   116	  // every exit path — the exit code stays governed by hook-safety.
   117	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ env, /** @type {number} */ code) => {
   118	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, env);
   119	    return code;
   120	  };
   121	
   122	  /** @type {any} */
   123	  let raw;
   124	  try {
   125	    raw = JSON.parse(typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '');
   126	  } catch {
   127	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: stdin could not be parsed as JSON — input ignored\n');
   128	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-stdin', msg: 'stdin could not be parsed as JSON' } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   129	  }
   130	
   131	  const session = { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime };
   132	  const events = normalizeHookPayload(raw, platform, session);
   133	  if (events.length === 0) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false } }, 0);
   134	
   135	  // Opt-in transcript tail (gate-2 fix 10): rides the journal as its own
   136	  // IMPORTANT event so the snapshot rewrite below persists it, and purge can
   137	  // strip it from every retained copy by its property name.
   138	  const tail = captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io);
   139	  if (tail !== null) {
   140	    events.push({
   141	      type: 'transcript.set',
   142	      payload: { transcript: { capturedAt: io.now(), tail } },
   143	      source: platform,
   144	      sessionHint: events[0]?.sessionHint,
   145	      unstable: events[0]?.unstable ?? true,
   146	    });
   147	  }
   148	
   149	  const { bundle, warnings, unsafe } = loadBundle(root, io);
   150	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${w}\n`);
   151	
   152	  // An unsafe managed tree (symlinked .handoff — gate-2 fix 6) is NOT "no
   153	  // bundle yet": seeding here would write through the link. Refuse everything;
   154	  // hook-safety keeps the refusal soft outside --strict.
   155	  if (unsafe === true) {
   156	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: managed tree failed the symlink/realpath jail — nothing written\n');
   157	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'unsafe-tree', msg: warnings[0] ?? 'managed tree failed the jail check' } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   158	  }
   159	
   160	  // Opt-in debounce (gate-2 major 14; Cursor afterFileEdit fires per edit):
   161	  // at most one real checkpoint per window, tracked by a per-platform stamp.
   162	  // The read-and-set runs UNDER the repo lock (gate-2 iter-2 M5) so two
   163	  // simultaneous afterFileEdit hooks can't both observe no stamp and both
   164	  // checkpoint — exactly one wins the window.
   165	  if (flags.debounce !== undefined) {
   166	    const seconds = Number(flags.debounce);
   167	    if (!Number.isFinite(seconds) || seconds <= 0) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--debounce <seconds> must be a positive number');
   168	    const stampPath = `${paths.logDir}/debounce-${platform}.json`;
   169	    const proceed = withLock(root, io, () => {
   170	      const stamp = safeReadJson(io.fs, stampPath);
   171	      if (stamp.ok && typeof stamp.value?.at === 'string' && Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(stamp.value.at) < seconds * 1000) {
   172	        return false;
   173	      }
   174	      ensureDir(io.fs, paths.logDir);
   175	      atomicWriteJson(io.fs, stampPath, { at: io.now() });
   176	      return true;
   177	    });
   178	    if (!proceed) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false, debounced: true } }, 0);
   179	  }
   180	
   181	  let active = bundle;
   182	  let mustRewrite = false;
   183	
   184	  // Semantic isolation (plan §Concurrency): a stable foreign session or a
   185	  // mismatched origin platform must not merge into the active bundle. Checked
   186	  // before ANY write so a rejection leaves the whole tree untouched.
   187	  const originHint = active?.origin?.sessionHint;
   188	  const hasStableOwner = active !== null && typeof originHint === 'string' && originHint.length > 0 && active.origin.unstable !== true;
   189	  const stableIncoming = events.find(
   190	    (/** @type {any} */ e) => typeof e.sessionHint === 'string' && e.sessionHint.length > 0 && e.unstable === false,
   191	  );
   192	  const unstableIncoming = events.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => e.unstable === true);
   193	
   194	  if (hasStableOwner && stableIncoming) {
   195	    const foreign = stableIncoming.sessionHint !== originHint || platform !== active.origin.platform;
   196	    if (foreign && flags['take-over'] !== true) {
   197	      io.stderr.write(
   198	        `baton checkpoint: event from a foreign session/origin (${platform}/${stableIncoming.sessionHint} vs active ${active.origin.platform}/${originHint}) — rejected to keep sessions isolated; rerun with --take-over to archive the active bundle and start fresh\n`,
   199	      );
   200	      return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'foreign-session', msg: 'event from a foreign session/origin rejected; rerun with --take-over' } }, 0);
   201	    }
   202	    if (foreign) {
   203	      rotateJournal(root, 'takeover', io);
   204	      const fresh = emptyBundle(
   205	        { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: active.task?.goal ?? 'unknown' },
   206	        io.now(),
   207	      );
   208	      fresh.origin.sessionHint = stableIncoming.sessionHint;
   209	      writeSnapshot(root, fresh, io);
   210	      io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: took over — prior bundle archived to history/, fresh bundle owned by ${platform}/${stableIncoming.sessionHint}\n`);
   211	      active = fresh;
   212	      mustRewrite = true;
   213	    }
   214	  }
   215	
   216	  if (unstableIncoming) {
   217	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: event carries an unstable (generated) session hint — ownership unverifiable; applied with this warning only\n');
   218	  }
   219	
   220	  if (active === null) {
   221	    active = emptyBundle(
   222	      { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: 'unknown' },
   223	      io.now(),
   224	    );
   225	    writeSnapshot(root, active, io);
   226	    // Self-applying seed event (gate-2 iter-2 B6): a journal-only rebuild
   227	    // (snapshot + .bak both lost) must restore the bundle's identity, not the
   228	    // "unknown" placeholder resolveBase seeds. bundle.seed carries bundleId,
   229	    // origin, task, and generation and is applied on replay.
   230	    appendJournal(
   231	      root,
   232	      {
   233	        ts: io.now(),
   234	        type: 'bundle.seed',
   235	        payload: {
   236	          bundleId: active.bundleId,
   237	          generation: active.generation,
   238	          createdAt: active.createdAt,
   239	          origin: active.origin,
   240	          task: { goal: active.task.goal },
   241	          text: `[seed] auto-seeded a new bundle on ${platform} (model ${active.origin.model})`,
   242	        },
   243	        writerId: [platform, io.pid, 'seed'].join('-'),
   244	        dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts: io.now(), type: 'bundle.seed', bundleId: active.bundleId }),
   245	      },
   246	      io,
   247	    );
   248	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: no bundle found — auto-seeded a new one at .handoff/bundle.json\n');
   249	    mustRewrite = true;
   250	  }
   251	
   252	  // Ownership adoption (gate-2 fix 2): a bundle whose owner is unverified — a
   253	  // fresh seed (null hint) or a receive that defaulted to an unstable hint —
   254	  // adopts the first STABLE same-platform session as its owner, so semantic
   255	  // isolation engages from then on instead of never.
   256	  const ownerUnverified = active.origin.sessionHint === null || active.origin.unstable === true;
   257	  if (ownerUnverified && stableIncoming && active.origin.platform === platform) {
   258	    active = { ...active, origin: { ...active.origin, sessionHint: stableIncoming.sessionHint, unstable: false } };
   259	    writeSnapshot(root, active, io);
   260	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: adopted ${platform}/${stableIncoming.sessionHint} as the bundle owner (first verified session)\n`);
   261	    mustRewrite = true;
   262	  }
   263	
   264	  let lastSeq = 0;
   265	  for (const ev of events) {
   266	    const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
   267	    const entry = {
   268	      ...ev,
   269	      ts,
   270	      dedupeKey:
   271	        typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0
   272	          ? ev.dedupeKey
   273	          : dedupeKey({ ts, type: ev.type, payload: ev.payload, source: ev.source, sessionHint: ev.sessionHint }),
   274	      writerId: [platform, io.pid, ev.sessionHint ?? 'local'].join('-'),
   275	    };
   276	    lastSeq = appendJournal(root, entry, io);
   277	  }
   278	
   279	  const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, paths.snapshot);
   280	  const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   281	  const snapUpdated = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.updatedAt === 'string' ? Date.parse(snap.value.updatedAt) : 0;
   282	  const important = events.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => !ROUTINE_TYPES.has(e.type));
   283	  const elapsed = Date.parse(io.now()) - snapUpdated > THROTTLE_WINDOW_MS;
   284	  const accreted = lastSeq - snapSeq;
   285	
   286	  let rewritten = false;
   287	  if (mustRewrite || important || elapsed || accreted >= THROTTLE_EVENT_COUNT) {
   288	    const merged = loadBundle(root, io).bundle;
   289	    if (merged) {
   290	      // Bounded git refresh on EVERY snapshot rewrite (gate-2 iter-2 M1): a
   291	      // mechanical checkpoint is defined to carry branch/HEAD/dirty, so any
   292	      // rewrite — routine Stop that cleared the throttle included, not only
   293	      // "important" events — refreshes git; unavailability degrades to the
   294	      // previous git state (or null), never a failure.
   295	      const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   296	      if (git !== null) merged.git = git;
   297	      writeSnapshot(root, merged, io);
   298	      rewritten = true;
   299	    }
   300	  }
   301	  return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: events.length, rewritten } }, 0);
   302	}
     1	/**
     2	 * Pure, explainable role resolution over the committed matrix. Every decision
     3	 * is auditable: chosen entries carry their chainIndex; passed-over entries land
     4	 * in skipped[] with a why. avoid[] population is the caller's job.
     5	 * @param {{config: any, to: string, avoid?: string[], nativeOnly?: boolean, probes?: Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}} input
     6	 * @returns {{assignments: Record<string, any>, notes: string[]}}
     7	 */
     8	export function resolveRoles({ config, to, avoid = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null }) {
     9	  /** @type {string[]} */
    10	  const notes = [];
    11	
    12	  /**
    13	   * The eligibility verdict for a platform, or null when eligible.
    14	   * @param {string} platform @returns {string | null}
    15	   */
    16	  function skipReason(platform) {
    17	    if (avoid.includes(platform)) return 'avoided';
    18	    if (!(platform in (config.platforms ?? {}))) return 'unknown-platform';
    19	    if (config.platforms[platform]?.enabled === false) return 'disabled';
    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
    30	    }
    31	    return null;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  // A selection is degraded when its availability is UNVERIFIED (gate-2 iter-2
    35	  // M3, iter-3 F1): with no probe cache at all, resolution is offline and EVERY
    36	  // selection is degraded (plan: "Offline resolution always succeeds, flagged
    37	  // degraded"); with a partial cache, platforms the cache is missing are; and a
    38	  // platform probed only to 'installed' (auth unverified, e.g. a --version
    39	  // success) is degraded even though it stays selectable.
    40	  /** @param {string} platform */
    41	  const probeUnverified = (platform) => probes == null || !probes[platform] || probes[platform].capability === 'installed';
    42	
    43	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    44	  const assignments = {};
    45	  for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(config.roles)) {
    46	    /** @type {any[]} */
    47	    const skipped = [];
    48	    /** @type {any} */
    49	    let assignment = null;
    50	
    51	    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    52	      const entry = chain[i];
    53	      if (nativeOnly && entry.platform !== to) {
    54	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'non-native' });
    55	        continue;
    56	      }
    57	      const why = skipReason(entry.platform);
    58	      if (why) {
    59	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why });
    60	        continue;
    61	      }
    62	      assignment = {
    63	        platform: entry.platform,
    64	        model: entry.model,
    65	        effort: entry.effort ?? null,
    66	        mode: entry.platform === to ? 'native' : 'delegated',
    67	        chainIndex: i,
    68	        skipped,
    69	      };
    70	      if (probeUnverified(entry.platform)) assignment.degraded = true;
    71	      break;
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    if (!assignment && nativeOnly) {
    75	      // Forced-default is only valid when the destination itself is eligible.
    76	      const whyTo = skipReason(to);
    77	      const model = config.defaults?.[to];
    78	      if (!whyTo && typeof model === 'string') {
    79	        assignment = { platform: to, model, effort: null, mode: 'forced-default', chainIndex: null, skipped };
    80	        if (probeUnverified(to)) assignment.degraded = true;
    81	      }
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    if (!assignment) {
    85	      assignment = { platform: null, model: null, effort: null, mode: 'unavailable', chainIndex: null, skipped };
    86	    }
    87	    assignments[role] = assignment;
    88	  }
    89	
    90	  return { assignments, notes };
    91	}
     1	import { atomicWriteJson } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
     2	import { appendEntry, readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
     3	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
     4	import { checkHandoffTree } from '../util/jail.mjs';
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * Operation-scoped mutation lock (plan §Concurrency, Lock model).
     8	 * Acquire = atomic non-recursive mkdir of .handoff/lock/; exclusivity comes from
     9	 * EEXIST. Ownership facts live in owner.json; logical session ownership lives in
    10	 * bundle.json, never here. Takeover only when the owner is provably dead on this
    11	 * host — every other contended state refuses, and `force` never changes that.
    12	 */
    13	
    14	export class LockHeldError extends Error {
    15	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
    16	    super(message);
    17	    this.name = 'LockHeldError';
    18	  }
    19	}
    20	
    21	export class FencingError extends Error {
    22	  constructor(/** @type {string} */ message) {
    23	    super(message);
    24	    this.name = 'FencingError';
    25	  }
    26	}
    27	
    28	/** @param {string} root */
    29	function lockPaths(root) {
    30	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
    31	  return {
    32	    dir,
    33	    lockDir: `${dir}/lock`,
    34	    owner: `${dir}/lock/owner.json`,
    35	    journal: `${dir}/journal.ndjson`,
    36	  };
    37	}
    38	
    39	const ESCAPE_HATCH =
    40	  'Verify manually that no baton process still holds it, then delete .handoff/lock to clear.';
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * Inspect the current lock state.
    44	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p
    45	 * @returns {{state: 'free'} | {state: 'torn', cause: string} | {state: 'live'|'dead'|'cross-host', owner: any}}
    46	 */
    47	function inspect(io, p) {
    48	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.lockDir)) return { state: 'free' };
    49	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.owner)) {
    50	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is missing (torn lock metadata)' };
    51	  }
    52	  let owner;
    53	  try {
    54	    owner = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
    55	  } catch {
    56	    return { state: 'torn', cause: 'owner.json is unparseable (torn lock metadata)' };
    57	  }
    58	  if (owner.host !== io.host) return { state: 'cross-host', owner };
    59	  if (io.processAlive(owner.pid, owner.startTime)) return { state: 'live', owner };
    60	  return { state: 'dead', owner };
    61	}
    62	
    63	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} text */
    64	function journalNote(io, p, text) {
    65	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
    66	  // Allocate seq + dedupeKey like every other journal entry (gate-2 fix,
    67	  // reviewer-b finding 2): a keyless, seq-less note poisons journalSeq to NaN
    68	  // on replay and dedupes every later keyless event. We are already inside the
    69	  // lock here, so reading the tail for allocation is race-free.
    70	  let tailSeq = 0;
    71	  try {
    72	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(`${p.dir}/bundle.json`, 'utf8'));
    73	    if (typeof snap?.journalSeq === 'number') tailSeq = snap.journalSeq;
    74	  } catch {
    75	    // no snapshot yet — journal tail below still counts
    76	  }
    77	  try {
    78	    for (const e of readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal).entries) {
    79	      if (typeof e.seq === 'number' && e.seq > tailSeq) tailSeq = e.seq;
    80	    }
    81	  } catch {
    82	    // no journal yet
    83	  }
    84	  const ts = io.now();
    85	  appendEntry(io.fs, p.journal, {
    86	    seq: tailSeq + 1,
    87	    ts,
    88	    type: 'note',
    89	    dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts, type: 'note', source: 'lock', text }),
    90	    writerId: ['lock', io.host, io.pid].join('-'),
    91	    source: 'lock',
    92	    payload: { text },
    93	  });
    94	}
    95	
    96	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
    97	function publishOwner(io, p) {
    98	  const token = io.newFencingToken();
    99	  atomicWriteJson(io.fs, p.owner, {
   100	    host: io.host,
   101	    pid: io.pid,
   102	    startTime: io.startTime,
   103	    fencingToken: token,
   104	    acquiredAt: io.now(),
   105	    heartbeatAt: io.now(),
   106	  });
   107	  return token;
   108	}
   109	
   110	/** @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p */
   111	function acquire(io, p) {
   112	  io.fs.mkdirSync(p.dir, { recursive: true });
   113	  try {
   114	    io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   115	    return publishOwner(io, p);
   116	  } catch (err) {
   117	    if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code !== 'EEXIST') throw err;
   118	  }
   119	
   120	  const s = inspect(io, p);
   121	  switch (s.state) {
   122	    case 'free':
   123	      // Raced a release between mkdir failure and inspection; retry once. A
   124	      // competitor can win THIS mkdir too (gate-2 fix 12: observed under real
   125	      // barrier-released contention) — that is contention, not corruption.
   126	      try {
   127	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   128	      } catch (err) {
   129	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
   130	          throw new LockHeldError('lock was re-acquired by a competitor mid-inspection — transient contention');
   131	        }
   132	        throw err;
   133	      }
   134	      return publishOwner(io, p);
   135	    case 'live':
   136	      throw new LockHeldError(
   137	        `lock is held by a live process (pid ${s.owner.pid} on ${s.owner.host}) — terminate that process first.`,
   138	      );
   139	    case 'cross-host':
   140	      throw new Error(
   141	        `lock is held from another host ('${s.owner.host}', this is '${io.host}') — cross-host locks are never provably safe to steal. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   142	      );
   143	    case 'torn':
   144	      // During ACQUIRE, torn metadata is usually a competitor caught between
   145	      // its mkdir and owner.json publication — transient by construction, so
   146	      // LockHeldError lets withLock wait it out (gate-2 fix 12). A genuinely
   147	      // crashed publisher exhausts the retry budget and surfaces this same
   148	      // message; recovery semantics (recoverLock) are unchanged: never steal.
   149	      throw new LockHeldError(`${s.cause} — unsupported for automatic recovery. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`);
   150	    case 'dead': {
   151	      // Atomic dead-lock reclaim (gate-2 iter-2 B1, hardened iter-3 finding-1).
   152	      // rm-then-mkdir is NOT mutually exclusive: two contenders can both observe
   153	      // 'dead' before either removes the stale dir, then both rm+mkdir — the
   154	      // second silently clobbers the first, yielding two simultaneous holders and
   155	      // two takeover notes. rename() is the atomic arbiter instead: the stale
   156	      // lock dir has exactly one name, so only one process can move it aside (the
   157	      // rest get ENOENT). Winning the rename IS winning the reclaim; a contender
   158	      // that recreated the dir via the 'free' path first makes our mkdir EEXIST →
   159	      // transient contention. The fencing token stays the write-time backstop.
   160	      const aside = `${p.lockDir}.reclaim-${io.pid}-${io.newFencingToken()}`;
   161	      try {
   162	        io.fs.renameSync(p.lockDir, aside);
   163	      } catch (err) {
   164	        // Source already gone → another contender is reclaiming it; wait+retry.
   165	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'ENOENT') {
   166	          throw new LockHeldError('a competitor is reclaiming the dead lock — transient contention');
   167	        }
   168	        throw err;
   169	      }
   170	      io.fs.rmSync(aside, { recursive: true, force: true });
   171	      try {
   172	        io.fs.mkdirSync(p.lockDir);
   173	      } catch (err) {
   174	        if (/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code === 'EEXIST') {
   175	          throw new LockHeldError('a competitor re-claimed the dead lock first — transient contention');
   176	        }
   177	        throw err;
   178	      }
   179	      const token = publishOwner(io, p);
   180	      // Journal the takeover only now — we HOLD the lock (fresh dir + published
   181	      // owner), so the note's seq is allocated while holding it (plan
   182	      // §Concurrency "sequence numbers are allocated under the lock"), never
   183	      // racing a free-path competitor's concurrent append (iter-3 F2).
   184	      // Journaling at the earlier rename win ran WITHOUT the lock and could
   185	      // collide seq with a real event → replay drops one. The note is therefore
   186	      // best-effort (0-or-1: lost only when we lose the fresh-mkdir race and
   187	      // throw above); the reclaim itself stays exactly-once via the atomic rename.
   188	      journalNote(io, p, `lock takeover: took over stale lock from dead pid ${s.owner.pid} (start time ${s.owner.startTime})`);
   189	      return token;
   190	    }
   191	    /* c8 ignore next 2 */
   192	    default:
   193	      throw new Error('unreachable lock state');
   194	  }
   195	}
   196	
   197	/**
   198	 * Fence-guarded release: the lock dir is removed only while owner.json still
   199	 * carries OUR token. A holder that paused past a takeover must not delete the
   200	 * competitor's lock on its way out — release is a write like any other.
   201	 * @param {any} io @param {ReturnType<typeof lockPaths>} p @param {string} token
   202	 */
   203	function release(io, p, token) {
   204	  try {
   205	    const current = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
   206	    if (current.fencingToken !== token) return; // a competitor took over — their lock, not ours
   207	  } catch {
   208	    return; // owner metadata gone or torn mid-hold — never guess; leave it for recovery
   209	  }
   210	  io.fs.rmSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   211	}
   212	
   213	// A live same-host holder is transient by design — operation-scoped locks are
   214	// held for milliseconds — so contention waits instead of dropping the
   215	// operation (gate-2 fix 12: simultaneous checkpoints must all land). The budget
   216	// is generous (≈15 s): under heavy load a writer may be starved of scheduling
   217	// for seconds, and waiting is strictly safer than silently dropping its work.
   218	// Every other contended state (torn, cross-host) stays fail-fast: waiting
   219	// cannot make those provably safe.
   220	const DEFAULT_RETRY = { attempts: 600, delayMs: 25 };
   221	
   222	/** Synchronous sleep without burning a core: Atomics.wait on a throwaway buffer. */
   223	const sleepMs = (/** @type {number} */ ms) => {
   224	  Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, ms);
   225	};
   226	
   227	/**
   228	 * Acquire → run fn(token) → release (also on throw). Synchronous by design:
   229	 * everything the lock guards is sync fs work. Live-held locks are retried on
   230	 * a bounded budget (override via io.lockRetry = {attempts, delayMs}).
   231	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {(token: string) => any} fn
   232	 */
   233	export function withLock(root, io, fn) {
   234	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   235	  // Jail BEFORE any lock mutation (gate-2 iter-2 B2): a symlinked .handoff or
   236	  // .handoff/lock must never be created into or removed through — that would
   237	  // reach outside the repository. Checked once here, ahead of acquire.
   238	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   239	  if (!safe.ok) throw new Error(`${safe.problem} — refusing to acquire the lock`);
   240	  const retry = io.lockRetry ?? DEFAULT_RETRY;
   241	  /** @type {string} */
   242	  let token;
   243	  for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt++) {
   244	    try {
   245	      token = acquire(io, p);
   246	      break;
   247	    } catch (err) {
   248	      if (err instanceof LockHeldError && attempt < retry.attempts) {
   249	        sleepMs(retry.delayMs);
   250	        continue;
   251	      }
   252	      throw err;
   253	    }
   254	  }
   255	  try {
   256	    return fn(token);
   257	  } finally {
   258	    release(io, p, token);
   259	  }
   260	}
   261	
   262	/**
   263	 * Fast-abort layer: re-read the published token immediately before a write; a
   264	 * holder that paused past its last check aborts instead of clobbering.
   265	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {string} token @param {() => any} fn
   266	 */
   267	export function guardedWrite(root, io, token, fn) {
   268	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   269	  let current = null;
   270	  try {
   271	    current = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(p.owner, 'utf8'));
   272	  } catch {
   273	    throw new FencingError('lock owner metadata is gone — fencing check failed; aborting write');
   274	  }
   275	  if (current.fencingToken !== token) {
   276	    throw new FencingError(
   277	      `fencing token changed (held '${token}', current '${current.fencingToken}') — another process took over; aborting write`,
   278	    );
   279	  }
   280	  return fn();
   281	}
   282	
   283	/**
   284	 * Explicit recovery. `force` is an intent flag only — it never changes the
   285	 * decision: exactly one state recovers (provably-dead same-host owner).
   286	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io @param {{force?: boolean}} [opts]
   287	 * @returns {{recovered: boolean, refusedReason: string | null}}
   288	 */
   289	export function recoverLock(root, io, opts = {}) {
   290	  void opts;
   291	  const p = lockPaths(root);
   292	  // Jail before recovery (gate-2 iter-2 B2): recoverLock removes the lock dir,
   293	  // so a symlinked managed tree must be refused first — even with force, a
   294	  // symlink is never provably safe to delete through.
   295	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   296	  if (!safe.ok) return { recovered: false, refusedReason: `${safe.problem} — refusing recovery` };
   297	  const s = inspect(io, p);
   298	  switch (s.state) {
   299	    case 'free':
   300	      return { recovered: true, refusedReason: null };
   301	    case 'dead': {
   302	      // Provably-dead owner: removal is justified by the verified state itself,
   303	      // not by holding the fence (there is no live holder to fence against).
   304	      io.fs.rmSync(p.lockDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   305	      // Journal the recovery under a fresh acquisition of the now-free lock so
   306	      // its seq is allocated while holding the lock (iter-3 F2) — journaling
   307	      // right after the bare rmSync ran without the lock and could collide seq
   308	      // with a writer that acquired in the gap. Best-effort: the recovery has
   309	      // already succeeded, so a note-write failure must not fail it.
   310	      const deadPid = s.owner.pid;
   311	      try {
   312	        withLock(root, io, () => journalNote(io, p, `lock recovery: cleared stale lock of dead pid ${deadPid}`));
   313	      } catch {
   314	        /* audit note is best-effort */
   315	      }
   316	      return { recovered: true, refusedReason: null };
   317	    }
   318	    case 'live':
   319	      return {
   320	        recovered: false,
   321	        refusedReason: `lock is held by a live process (pid ${s.owner.pid} on ${s.owner.host}) — terminate that process first.`,
   322	      };
   323	    case 'cross-host':
   324	      return {
   325	        recovered: false,
   326	        refusedReason: `lock is held from another host ('${s.owner.host}', this is '${io.host}') — cross-host locks are never provably safe to steal, even with force. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   327	      };
   328	    case 'torn':
   329	      return {
   330	        recovered: false,
   331	        refusedReason: `${s.cause} — unsupported for automatic recovery, even with force. ${ESCAPE_HATCH}`,
   332	      };
   333	    /* c8 ignore next 2 */
   334	    default:
   335	      return { recovered: false, refusedReason: 'unreachable lock state' };
   336	  }
   337	}

codex
Initial code reading has exposed two places where the disposition may be incomplete, so I’m testing them directly rather than treating the new helper/tests as sufficient: UNC auto-discovery loses the UNC prefix during normalization, and resolver selections with a non-OK probe outcome above `installed` still appear not to receive `degraded`. I’ll confirm both against the plan and surrounding behavior before assigning severity.

tests/commands/checkpoint-pipeline-2.test.mjs:13:// - M5: the debounce stamp check-and-set runs UNDER the repo lock, so two
tests/commands/checkpoint-pipeline-2.test.mjs:32:function seededBundle(updatedAt) {
tests/commands/checkpoint-pipeline-2.test.mjs:65:describe('M5 — debounce stamp check-and-set is serialized under the lock', () => {
     1	/**
     2	 * Pure, explainable role resolution over the committed matrix. Every decision
     3	 * is auditable: chosen entries carry their chainIndex; passed-over entries land
     4	 * in skipped[] with a why. avoid[] population is the caller's job.
     5	 * @param {{config: any, to: string, avoid?: string[], nativeOnly?: boolean, probes?: Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}} input
     6	 * @returns {{assignments: Record<string, any>, notes: string[]}}
     7	 */
     8	export function resolveRoles({ config, to, avoid = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null }) {
     9	  /** @type {string[]} */
    10	  const notes = [];
    11	
    12	  /**
    13	   * The eligibility verdict for a platform, or null when eligible.
    14	   * @param {string} platform @returns {string | null}
    15	   */
    16	  function skipReason(platform) {
    17	    if (avoid.includes(platform)) return 'avoided';
    18	    if (!(platform in (config.platforms ?? {}))) return 'unknown-platform';
    19	    if (config.platforms[platform]?.enabled === false) return 'disabled';
    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
    30	    }
    31	    return null;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  // A selection is degraded when its availability is UNVERIFIED (gate-2 iter-2
    35	  // M3, iter-3 F1): with no probe cache at all, resolution is offline and EVERY
    36	  // selection is degraded (plan: "Offline resolution always succeeds, flagged
    37	  // degraded"); with a partial cache, platforms the cache is missing are; and a
    38	  // platform probed only to 'installed' (auth unverified, e.g. a --version
    39	  // success) is degraded even though it stays selectable.
    40	  /** @param {string} platform */
    41	  const probeUnverified = (platform) => probes == null || !probes[platform] || probes[platform].capability === 'installed';
    42	
    43	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    44	  const assignments = {};
    45	  for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(config.roles)) {
    46	    /** @type {any[]} */
    47	    const skipped = [];
    48	    /** @type {any} */
    49	    let assignment = null;
    50	
    51	    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    52	      const entry = chain[i];
    53	      if (nativeOnly && entry.platform !== to) {
    54	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'non-native' });
    55	        continue;
    56	      }
    57	      const why = skipReason(entry.platform);
    58	      if (why) {
    59	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why });
    60	        continue;
    61	      }
    62	      assignment = {
    63	        platform: entry.platform,
    64	        model: entry.model,
    65	        effort: entry.effort ?? null,
    66	        mode: entry.platform === to ? 'native' : 'delegated',
    67	        chainIndex: i,
    68	        skipped,
    69	      };
    70	      if (probeUnverified(entry.platform)) assignment.degraded = true;
    71	      break;
    72	    }
    73	
    74	    if (!assignment && nativeOnly) {
    75	      // Forced-default is only valid when the destination itself is eligible.
    76	      const whyTo = skipReason(to);
    77	      const model = config.defaults?.[to];
    78	      if (!whyTo && typeof model === 'string') {
    79	        assignment = { platform: to, model, effort: null, mode: 'forced-default', chainIndex: null, skipped };
    80	        if (probeUnverified(to)) assignment.degraded = true;
    81	      }
    82	    }
    83	
    84	    if (!assignment) {
    85	      assignment = { platform: null, model: null, effort: null, mode: 'unavailable', chainIndex: null, skipped };
    86	    }
    87	    assignments[role] = assignment;
    88	  }
    89	
    90	  return { assignments, notes };
    91	}
     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { resolveRoles } from '../../core/src/roles/resolve.mjs';
     4	
     5	// ===========================================================================
     6	// Contract choices for roles/resolve.mjs. Source of truth: docs/design/core.md
     7	// §Module APIs (resolveRoles signature + assignment shape); plan §Core engine —
     8	// Role matrix + Availability probing (capability/outcome dimensions, avoid[],
     9	// native/delegated/forced-default/unavailable, chainIndex + skipped audit data).
    10	// resolveRoles is PURE and explainable.
    11	//
    12	// INPUT: resolveRoles({config, to, avoid = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null}).
    13	//   `config` is the PARSED config (roles as arrays of {platform, model, effort},
    14	//   as loadConfig returns) — built inline here so this file's red phase is
    15	//   attributable to resolve.mjs alone (not matrix.mjs).
    16	//
    17	// RETURN: { assignments: {[role]: Assignment}, notes: [] }.
    18	//   Assignment = {platform, model, effort, mode, chainIndex, skipped[], degraded?}.
    19	//   modes: 'native' | 'delegated' | 'forced-default' | 'unavailable'.
    20	//
    21	// PINS:
    22	//   1. CHAIN WALK. For each role, walk entries in order; the FIRST eligible
    23	//      (surviving) entry is selected. skipped[] records each passed-over entry
    24	//      as {chainIndex, platform, model, why} (extra fields tolerated).
    25	//      why values: 'avoided' | 'unknown-platform' | 'disabled' | 'rate-limited' | 'unauthenticated'.
    26	//   2. ELIGIBILITY / skip reasons:
    27	//        - platform ∈ avoid                          -> skip, why 'avoided'
    28	//        - platform NOT a key of config.platforms    -> skip, why 'unknown-platform'
    29	//        - config.platforms[p].enabled === false     -> skip, why 'disabled'
    30	//        - probes given & probes[p].outcome==='rate-limited' -> skip, why 'rate-limited'
    31	//        - probes given & capability==='installed' AND outcome!=='ok' (a VERIFIED
    32	//          auth/reachability FAILURE)                 -> skip, why 'unauthenticated'
    33	//          (iter-3 F1: a capped SUCCESS {installed, ok} leaves auth UNVERIFIED —
    34	//          it stays selectable and is flagged degraded, it is NOT skipped)
    35	//      A platform with capability ∈ {authenticated, reachable} AND outcome
    36	//      ≠ 'rate-limited' is eligible.
    37	//   3. DEGRADED. If probes is a non-null object but has NO entry for the chosen
    38	//      platform (unverifiable), the entry is selectable with degraded === true.
    39	//      probes === null means OFFLINE resolution: still selectable, but every
    40	//      selection is flagged degraded (gate-2 iter-2 M3; plan: offline always degraded).
    41	//      Non-degraded assignments have a falsy `degraded` (absent or false).
    42	//   4. MODE. First surviving entry: mode 'native' iff entry.platform === to,
    43	//      else 'delegated'. chainIndex = that entry's index in the ORIGINAL chain.
    44	//   5. nativeOnly restricts consideration to entries with platform === to; if
    45	//      none survive it MAY fall back to config.defaults[to] with mode
    46	//      'forced-default' (platform=to, model=defaults[to], effort=null,
    47	//      chainIndex=null) — but ONLY when the destination `to` is itself
    48	//      probe-eligible (PIN 2). When `to` is probe-INELIGIBLE (e.g. rate-limited),
    49	//      the forced-default is SUPPRESSED and the role resolves 'unavailable', the
    50	//      ineligibility audited in skipped[] (why 'rate-limited'). (fold C1-1)
    51	//   6. UNAVAILABLE. In normal (non-nativeOnly) mode, when NOTHING survives:
    52	//      mode 'unavailable', platform null, model null, effort null,
    53	//      chainIndex null, and skipped[] carries the full passed-over list.
    54	//   7. DETERMINISM. Same input -> deep-equal output; input config is not mutated.
    55	//      assignments object keys are in the CONFIG'S ROLE ORDER.
    56	//   8. avoid[] auto-population is the CALLER'S job; resolve only honors the list.
    57	// ===========================================================================
    58	
    59	const e = (platform, model, effort = null) => ({ platform, model, effort });
    60	
    61	const PLATFORMS = { 'claude-code': {}, codex: {}, cursor: {} };
    62	const DEFAULTS = { 'claude-code': 'claude-fable-5', codex: 'gpt-5.6-sol', cursor: 'composer' };
    63	
    64	// The full seven-role config in parsed form (mirrors the real baton.config.json).
    65	function fullConfig() {
    66	  return {
    67	    schema: 'baton/config@1',
    68	    roles: {
    69	      planner: [e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5')],
    70	      'plan-reviewer': [e('codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'xhigh'), e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5', 'xhigh')],
    71	      'test-author': [e('claude-code', 'claude-opus-4-8'), e('codex', 'gpt-5.5', 'xhigh')],
    72	      'test-verifier': [e('codex', 'gpt-5.5', 'xhigh'), e('claude-code', 'claude-opus-4-8')],
    73	      implementer: [e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5'), e('codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'xhigh'), e('cursor', 'composer')],
    74	      'final-reviewer-a': [e('codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'xhigh'), e('codex', 'gpt-5.5', 'xhigh')],
    75	      'final-reviewer-b': [e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5', 'xhigh'), e('claude-code', 'claude-opus-4-8')],
    76	    },
    77	    platforms: { ...PLATFORMS },
    78	    defaults: { ...DEFAULTS },
    79	  };
    80	}
    81	
    82	// A small single-role config helper.
    83	function oneRole(chain) {
    84	  return { schema: 'baton/config@1', roles: { r: chain }, platforms: { ...PLATFORMS }, defaults: { ...DEFAULTS } };
    85	}
    86	
    87	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

   100	    assert.equal(assignments.planner.skipped[0].platform, 'claude-code');
   101

   136	
   137	  function pick(a) {
   138	    return { platform: a.platform, model: a.model, effort: a.effort, mode: a.mode, chainIndex: a.chainIndex };
   139	  }
   140	});

   143	describe('resolveRoles — mode selection', () => {
   144	  it('native: first surviving entry whose platform === to', () => {
   145	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({ config: oneRole([e('codex', 'gpt-5.5', 'xhigh')]), to: 'codex' });
   146	    assert.equal(assignments.r.mode, 'native');
   147	    assert.equal(assignments.r.platform, 'codex');
   148	    assert.equal(assignments.r.chainIndex, 0);
   149	  });
   150

   161	
   162	  it('unavailable: normal mode, every entry skipped -> platform/model null, full skipped[], NO forced default', () => {
   163	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({
   164	      config: oneRole([e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5'), e('codex', 'gpt-5.5', 'xhigh')]),
   165	      to: 'codex',
   166	      avoid: ['claude-code', 'codex'],
   167	    });
   168	    assert.equal(assignments.r.mode, 'unavailable');
   169	    assert.equal(assignments.r.platform, null);
   170	    assert.equal(assignments.r.model, null);
   171	    assert.equal(assignments.r.effort, null);
   172	    assert.equal(assignments.r.chainIndex, null);
   173	    assert.equal(assignments.r.skipped.length, 2);
   174	  });
   175	});

   177	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   178	describe('resolveRoles — nativeOnly + forced-default', () => {
   179	  it('nativeOnly picks a native entry, skipping delegated ones', () => {
   180	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({
   181	      config: oneRole([e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5'), e('codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'xhigh')]),
   182	      to: 'codex',
   183	      nativeOnly: true,
   184	    });
   185	    assert.equal(assignments.r.mode, 'native');
   186	    assert.equal(assignments.r.platform, 'codex');
   187	    assert.equal(assignments.r.chainIndex, 1);
   188	  });
   189	
   190	  it('nativeOnly with no native entry falls back to defaults[to] as forced-default (no probing)', () => {
   191	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({
   192	      config: oneRole([e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5')]),
   193	      to: 'codex',
   194	      nativeOnly: true,
   195	    });
   196	    assert.equal(assignments.r.mode, 'forced-default');
   197	    assert.equal(assignments.r.platform, 'codex');
   198	    assert.equal(assignments.r.model, 'gpt-5.6-sol'); // defaults.codex
   199	    assert.equal(assignments.r.effort, null);
   200	    assert.equal(assignments.r.chainIndex, null);
   201	  });
   202	
   203	  it('nativeOnly forced-default requires the destination `to` to be probe-ELIGIBLE (authenticated/ok)', () => {
   204	    // No native codex entry in the chain, but codex (the destination) is probed
   205	    // eligible -> the forced-default IS selectable. This ties the forced-default
   206	    // fallback to destination probe-eligibility (fold C1-1).
   207	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({
   208	      config: oneRole([e('claude-code', 'claude-fable-5')]),
   209	      to: 'codex',
   210	      nativeOnly: true,
   211	      probes: { codex: { capability: 'authenticated', outcome: 'ok' } },
   212	    });
   213	    assert.equal(assignments.r.mode, 'forced-default');
   214	    assert.equal(assignments.r.platform, 'codex');
   215	    assert.equal(assignments.r.model, 'gpt-5.6-sol');
   216	    assert.equal(assignments.r.effort, null);
   217	    assert.equal(assignments.r.chainIndex, null);
   218	  });
   219	
   220	  it('nativeOnly: the only native entry (=to) is rate-limited -> forced-default SUPPRESSED, unavailable with a rate-limited skip', () => {
   221	    // The destination platform itself is rate-limited, so forcing its default is
   222	    // not valid: the role resolves 'unavailable', and the passed-over rate-limited
   223	    // entry is audited in skipped[] with why 'rate-limited' (fold C1-1, replaces
   224	    // the earlier test that wrongly selected a rate-limited forced-default).
   225	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({
   226	      config: oneRole([e('codex', 'gpt-5.6-sol', 'xhigh')]),
   227	      to: 'codex',
   228	      nativeOnly: true,
   229	      probes: { codex: { capability: 'authenticated', outcome: 'rate-limited' } },
   230	    });
   231	    assert.equal(assignments.r.mode, 'unavailable');
   232	    assert.equal(assignments.r.platform, null);
   233	    assert.equal(assignments.r.model, null);
   234	    assert.equal(assignments.r.effort, null);
   235	    assert.equal(assignments.r.chainIndex, null);
   236	    const rl = assignments.r.skipped.find((s) => s.why === 'rate-limited' && s.platform === 'codex');
   237	    assert.ok(rl, 'the rate-limited destination entry must be audited in skipped[] with why "rate-limited"');
   238	  });
   239	});

   241	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   242	describe('resolveRoles — skip reasons', () => {
   243	  it("unknown platform (absent from config.platforms) -> skip, why 'unknown-platform'", () => {
   244	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({

   253	
   254	  it("probe outcome 'rate-limited' -> skip, why 'rate-limited'", () => {
   255	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({
   256	      config: oneRole([e('codex', 'a'), e('cursor', 'b')]),
   257	      to: 'cursor',

   259	    });
   260	    assert.equal(assignments.r.platform, 'cursor');
   261	    assert.equal(assignments.r.chainIndex, 1);

   264	  });
   265	
   266	  it("capability 'installed' with a capped SUCCESS -> selectable + degraded, not skipped (iter-3 F1)", () => {
   267	    // A --version success caps capability at 'installed' but leaves auth
   268	    // UNVERIFIED (not verified-failed), so the platform stays selectable and is
   269	    // flagged degraded — per plan §Role matrix. Only a verified failure skips.
   270	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({
   271	      config: oneRole([e('codex', 'a'), e('cursor', 'b')]),
   272	      to: 'cursor',
   273	      probes: { codex: { capability: 'installed', outcome: 'ok' }, cursor: { capability: 'authenticated', outcome: 'ok' } },
   274	    });
   275	    assert.equal(assignments.r.platform, 'codex', 'auth-unverified codex stays selectable at chain head');
   276	    assert.equal(assignments.r.degraded, true, 'the auth-unverified selection is flagged degraded');
   277	    assert.equal(assignments.r.skipped.length, 0);
   278	  });
   279	
   280	  it("capability 'installed' with a VERIFIED failure -> skip, why 'unauthenticated' (iter-3 F1)", () => {
   281	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({
   282	      config: oneRole([e('codex', 'a'), e('cursor', 'b')]),
   283	      to: 'cursor',
   284	      probes: { codex: { capability: 'installed', outcome: 'error' }, cursor: { capability: 'authenticated', outcome: 'ok' } },
   285	    });
   286	    assert.equal(assignments.r.platform, 'cursor');
   287	    assert.equal(assignments.r.skipped[0].why, 'unauthenticated');
   288	    assert.equal(assignments.r.skipped[0].platform, 'codex');
   289	  });
   290	
   291	  it("a platform configured { enabled: false } is skipped, why 'disabled' (no probes needed) (fold C1-6)", () => {
   292	    // The disabled shape is config.platforms[p] = { enabled: false }. It is a KEY
   293	    // of config.platforms (so NOT 'unknown-platform'), but explicitly disabled, so
   294	    // it is skipped with why 'disabled' — distinct from avoid[]/rate-limited.
   295	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({
   296	      config: {
   297	        schema: 'baton/config@1',
   298	        roles: { r: [e('codex', 'a'), e('cursor', 'b')] },
   299	        platforms: { 'claude-code': {}, codex: { enabled: false }, cursor: {} },
   300	        defaults: { ...DEFAULTS },
   301	      },
   302	      to: 'cursor',
   303	    });
   304	    assert.equal(assignments.r.platform, 'cursor');
   305	    assert.equal(assignments.r.chainIndex, 1);
   306	    assert.equal(assignments.r.skipped.length, 1);
   307	    assert.equal(assignments.r.skipped[0].why, 'disabled');
   308	    assert.equal(assignments.r.skipped[0].platform, 'codex');
   309	  });
   310	
   311	  it("an { enabled: true } (or bare {}) platform is NOT disabled — only enabled===false disables", () => {
   312	    // Guards against the implementer treating any non-empty platform config as a
   313	    // disable signal: enabled:true must remain eligible.
   314	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({
   315	      config: {
   316	        schema: 'baton/config@1',
   317	        roles: { r: [e('codex', 'a')] },
   318	        platforms: { 'claude-code': {}, codex: { enabled: true }, cursor: {} },
   319	        defaults: { ...DEFAULTS },
   320	      },
   321	      to: 'codex',
   322	    });
   323	    assert.equal(assignments.r.mode, 'native');
   324	    assert.equal(assignments.r.platform, 'codex');
   325	    assert.equal(assignments.r.skipped.length, 0);
   326	  });
   327	});

   330	describe('resolveRoles — probe eligibility & degraded flag', () => {

     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { resolveRoles } from '../../core/src/roles/resolve.mjs';
     4	
     5	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     6	// Gate-2 iteration-3 F1 (reviewer-a #6, reviewer-b #1): doctor caps a healthy
     7	// `claude --version` probe at capability 'installed' (capOnSuccess), so the
     8	// probe cache doctor writes carries {capability:'installed', outcome:'ok'} for a
     9	// fully healthy claude-code. A version probe leaves AUTH UNVERIFIED — not
    10	// verified-failed — so the plan (§Role matrix) requires selectable-with-degraded,
    11	// NOT skipped-as-unauthenticated. The resolver must only skip 'unauthenticated'
    12	// on a VERIFIED auth failure (installed AND outcome !== 'ok').
    13	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14	
    15	const CONFIG = {
    16	  schema: 'baton/config@1',
    17	  roles: {
    18	    ccFirst: [{ platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5' }, { platform: 'codex', model: 'gpt-5.6-sol' }],
    19	    ccOnly: [{ platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5' }],
    20	  },
    21	  platforms: { 'claude-code': {}, codex: {}, cursor: {} },
    22	  defaults: { 'claude-code': 'claude-fable-5', codex: 'gpt-5.6-sol', cursor: 'composer' },
    23	};
    24	
    25	// The exact cache shape doctor writes on a healthy machine: claude-code capped
    26	// at 'installed' by capOnSuccess, codex/cursor probed to 'reachable'.
    27	const HEALTHY_CACHE = {
    28	  'claude-code': { capability: 'installed', outcome: 'ok' },
    29	  codex: { capability: 'reachable', outcome: 'ok' },
    30	  cursor: { capability: 'reachable', outcome: 'ok' },
    31	};
    32	
    33	describe('F1 — a healthy version-probed platform is selectable-but-degraded, not skipped', () => {
    34	  it('claude-code {installed, ok} is SELECTED (degraded) where first-eligible, not skipped as unauthenticated', () => {
    35	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({ config: CONFIG, to: 'codex', probes: HEALTHY_CACHE });
    36	    const cc = assignments.ccFirst;
    37	    assert.equal(cc.platform, 'claude-code', 'a version-probed claude-code stays selectable at chain head');
    38	    assert.equal(cc.degraded, true, 'auth-unverified selection carries the degraded flag');
    39	    assert.ok(!(cc.skipped ?? []).some((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === 'claude-code'), 'claude-code is not skipped');
    40	  });
    41	
    42	  it('a claude-code-only role resolves to claude-code (degraded), never "unavailable"', () => {
    43	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({ config: CONFIG, to: 'claude-code', probes: HEALTHY_CACHE });
    44	    assert.equal(assignments.ccOnly.mode, 'native');
    45	    assert.equal(assignments.ccOnly.platform, 'claude-code');
    46	    assert.equal(assignments.ccOnly.degraded, true);
    47	  });
    48	
    49	  it('a VERIFIED auth/reachability failure ({installed, error}) IS skipped as unauthenticated', () => {
    50	    const probes = { ...HEALTHY_CACHE, 'claude-code': { capability: 'installed', outcome: 'error' } };
    51	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({ config: CONFIG, to: 'codex', probes });
    52	    const cc = assignments.ccFirst;
    53	    assert.equal(cc.platform, 'codex', 'a verified-failed claude-code falls through to codex');
    54	    assert.ok((cc.skipped ?? []).some((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === 'claude-code' && s.why === 'unauthenticated'), 'recorded as unauthenticated');
    55	  });
    56	
    57	  it('a fully-reachable platform still resolves without a degraded flag', () => {
    58	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({ config: CONFIG, to: 'codex', probes: HEALTHY_CACHE });
    59	    // codex is reachable+ok → the delegated pick for a codex-native role is not degraded.
    60	    const codexPick = resolveRoles({ config: { ...CONFIG, roles: { r: [{ platform: 'codex', model: 'gpt-5.6-sol' }] } }, to: 'codex', probes: HEALTHY_CACHE }).assignments.r;
    61	    assert.equal(codexPick.degraded ?? false, false, 'a reachable+ok platform is not degraded');
    62	    assert.ok(assignments.ccFirst);
    63	  });
    64	});
     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { makeIo } from '../helpers/fakeio.mjs';
     4	import { normSep, joinNorm, isContained } from '../../core/src/util/pathnorm.mjs';
     5	import { checkHandoffTree } from '../../core/src/util/jail.mjs';
     6	import { resolveRoot } from '../../core/src/commands/shared.mjs';
     7	
     8	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     9	// Gate-2 iteration-3 F6 + F9 (reviewer-a #5/#10, reviewer-b #6): the Windows
    10	// path class. realpathSync returns backslash paths; every containment/ascent
    11	// check must compare in one normalized forward-slash space, and a drive-root
    12	// repo (C:/) must not produce a doubled-slash C://.handoff that never matches.
    13	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14	
    15	describe('normSep / joinNorm / isContained', () => {
    16	  it('normSep canonicalizes backslashes, doubled and trailing separators', () => {
    17	    assert.equal(normSep('C:\\repo\\.handoff'), 'C:/repo/.handoff');
    18	    assert.equal(normSep('C://repo//.handoff'), 'C:/repo/.handoff');
    19	    assert.equal(normSep('/repo/'), '/repo');
    20	    assert.equal(normSep('/'), '/');
    21	  });
    22	
    23	  it('joinNorm never doubles the separator at a drive root', () => {
    24	    assert.equal(joinNorm('C:/', '.handoff'), 'C:/.handoff');
    25	    assert.equal(joinNorm('/repo', '.handoff'), '/repo/.handoff');
    26	  });
    27	
    28	  it('isContained is volume-aware and prefix-safe', () => {
    29	    assert.equal(isContained('C:\\repo', 'C:/repo/.handoff'), true);
    30	    assert.equal(isContained('C:/repo', 'C:/repository'), false); // not a path-segment prefix
    31	    assert.equal(isContained('C:/', 'C:/.handoff'), true);
    32	    assert.equal(isContained('/repo', '/repo'), true);
    33	    assert.equal(isContained('/repo', '/other/.handoff'), false);
    34	  });
    35	});
    36	
    37	describe('checkHandoffTree containment on Windows realpaths (F6)', () => {
    38	  const DIR = 'C:/repo/.handoff';
    39	  const winIo = (rootReal, dirReal) => ({
    40	    fs: {
    41	      existsSync: (/** @type {any} */ p) => String(p) === DIR,
    42	      lstatSync: (/** @type {any} */ p) => ({
    43	        isSymbolicLink: () => false,
    44	        isDirectory: () => String(p) === DIR,
    45	        isFile: () => String(p) !== DIR,
    46	      }),
    47	      realpathSync: (/** @type {any} */ p) => {
    48	        const s = String(p);
    49	        if (s === 'C:/repo') return rootReal;
    50	        if (s === DIR) return dirReal;
    51	        return s;
    52	      },
    53	      readdirSync: () => [],
    54	    },
    55	  });
    56	
    57	  it('accepts a backslash-spelled in-tree .handoff', () => {
    58	    assert.deepEqual(checkHandoffTree('C:/repo', winIo('C:\\repo', 'C:\\repo\\.handoff')), { ok: true });
    59	  });
    60	
    61	  it('accepts a DRIVE-ROOT repo (C:/) without a doubled-slash mismatch', () => {
    62	    const io = {
    63	      fs: {
    64	        existsSync: (/** @type {any} */ p) => String(p) === 'C:/.handoff',
    65	        lstatSync: (/** @type {any} */ p) => ({ isSymbolicLink: () => false, isDirectory: () => String(p) === 'C:/.handoff', isFile: () => false }),
    66	        realpathSync: (/** @type {any} */ p) => (String(p) === 'C:/' ? 'C:\\' : String(p) === 'C:/.handoff' ? 'C:\\.handoff' : String(p)),
    67	        readdirSync: () => [],
    68	      },
    69	    };
    70	    assert.deepEqual(checkHandoffTree('C:/', io), { ok: true });
    71	  });
    72	
    73	  it('still refuses an escaped/cross-volume realpath', () => {
    74	    const r = checkHandoffTree('C:/repo', winIo('C:\\repo', 'D:\\repo\\.handoff'));
    75	    assert.equal(r.ok, false);
    76	    assert.match(r.problem, /outside the repository root/i);
    77	  });
    78	});
    79	
    80	describe('resolveRoot ascent examines the drive root (F6)', () => {
    81	  it('finds a marker at a drive-root repo C:/', () => {
    82	    // baton.config.json is a FILE (the memfs fake resolves store files by their
    83	    // normalized key; directory existence has a leading-slash quirk irrelevant
    84	    // to the code path under test — the drive-root ascent).
    85	    const io = makeIo({ files: { 'C:/baton.config.json': '{}', 'C:/sub/deep/x.txt': 'x' } });
    86	    io.cwd = 'C:\\sub\\deep';
    87	    assert.equal(resolveRoot(io, {}), 'C:/');
    88	  });
    89	
    90	  it('still resolves a nested drive-path repo', () => {
    91	    const io = makeIo({ files: { 'C:/repo/baton.config.json': '{}', 'C:/repo/sub/x.txt': 'x' } });
    92	    io.cwd = 'C:\\repo\\sub';
    93	    assert.equal(resolveRoot(io, {}), 'C:/repo');
    94	  });
    95	});

   374	describe('resolveRoles — determinism & shape', () => {
   375	  it('same input -> deep-equal output across two calls', () => {
   376	    const a = resolveRoles({ config: fullConfig(), to: 'codex', avoid: ['claude-code'] });
   377	    const b = resolveRoles({ config: fullConfig(), to: 'codex', avoid: ['claude-code'] });
   378	    assert.deepEqual(a, b);
   379	  });
   380	
   381	  it('does not mutate the input config', () => {
   382	    const config = fullConfig();
   383	    const snapshot = structuredClone(config);
   384	    resolveRoles({ config, to: 'codex', avoid: ['claude-code'], probes: { codex: { capability: 'reachable', outcome: 'ok' } } });
   385	    assert.deepEqual(config, snapshot, 'config must be left untouched');
   386	  });
   387	
   388	  it('assignments keys are in the config role order', () => {
   389	    const config = fullConfig();
   390	    const { assignments } = resolveRoles({ config, to: 'codex' });
   391	    assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(assignments), Object.keys(config.roles));
   392	  });
   393	
   394	  it('every assignment carries the documented fields; notes is an array', () => {
   395	    const { assignments, notes } = resolveRoles({ config: fullConfig(), to: 'codex', avoid: ['claude-code'] });
   396	    assert.ok(Array.isArray(notes), 'notes must be an array');
   397	    for (const [role, a] of Object.entries(assignments)) {
   398	      for (const key of ['platform', 'model', 'effort', 'mode', 'chainIndex', 'skipped']) {
   399	        assert.ok(key in a, `${role}.${key} must be present`);
   400	      }
   401	      assert.ok(Array.isArray(a.skipped), `${role}.skipped must be an array`);
   402	      assert.ok(['native', 'delegated', 'forced-default', 'unavailable'].includes(a.mode), `${role}.mode invalid: ${a.mode}`);
   403	    }
   404	  });
   405	});
     1	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     2	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     3	import * as realFs from 'node:fs';
     4	import { bundlePaths } from '../bundle/store.mjs';
     5	import { loadSignatures } from '../detect/signatures.mjs';
     6	import { classify } from '../detect/classifier.mjs';
     7	import { PROBE_CACHE_MS } from '../roles/availability.mjs';
     8	import { ensureDir, atomicWriteJson, safeReadJson } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
     9	import { readAllTolerant } from '../util/jsonl.mjs';
    10	import { checkHandoffTree } from '../util/jail.mjs';
    11	import { emitEnvelope, parseFlags, resolveRoot } from './shared.mjs';
