    92	  });
    93	
    94	  it('refuses to read the bundle through a symlinked .handoff — emits no context (F12)', async () => {
    95	    const io = hookIo(sealedForeign('codex'));
    96	    // Simulate .handoff being a symlink: checkHandoffTree must refuse before the
    97	    // raw bundle read, so sessionStart emits nothing and never follows the link.
    98	    const realLstat = io.fs.lstatSync.bind(io.fs);
    99	    io.fs.lstatSync = (p) => {
   100	      if (String(p) === '/repo/.handoff') return { ...realLstat('/repo/.handoff'), isSymbolicLink: () => true, isDirectory: () => true, isFile: () => false };
   101	      return realLstat(p);
   102	    };
   103	    let readThroughTree = false;
   104	    const realRead = io.fs.readFileSync.bind(io.fs);
   105	    io.fs.readFileSync = (p, enc) => {
   106	      if (String(p) === '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json') readThroughTree = true;
   107	      return realRead(p, enc);
   108	    };
   109	    const code = await runHook(['SessionStart'], io);
   110	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'fail-open');
   111	    assert.equal(io.stdoutText(), '', 'no context is injected from an unsafe tree');
   112	    assert.equal(readThroughTree, false, 'the bundle is never read through the symlinked tree');
   113	  });
   114	});
     1	// Single Claude Code hook entrypoint. hooks.json routes every event here with
     2	// the event name as argv[0]; this script's only job is to reach the core CLI
     3	// (never re-implement it) and to stay fail-open: a hook must never break the
     4	// host session, so every path returns 0.
     5	
     6	import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
     7	import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
     8	import * as nodeFs from 'node:fs';
     9	import { pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
    10	import { checkHandoffTree } from '../../../core/src/util/jail.mjs';
    11	
    12	/** @param {any} io */
    13	const batonBin = (io) => `${io.env?.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT ?? '.'}/core/bin/baton.mjs`;
    14	
    15	/**
    16	 * execFile-shaped spawn that supports {input}: async execFile has no input
    17	 * option, and a child reading stdin would otherwise block forever (gate-2
    18	 * reviewer-b finding 1). The io contract for this adapter is therefore
    19	 * "promise execFile honoring opts.input as the child's stdin".
    20	 * @param {string} cmd @param {string[]} args @param {{cwd?: string, input?: string, timeout?: number}} [opts]
    21	 * @returns {Promise<{stdout: string, stderr: string}>}
    22	 */
    23	function execFileWithInput(cmd, args = [], opts = {}) {
    24	  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    25	    const child = spawn(cmd, args, { cwd: opts.cwd, timeout: opts.timeout ?? 25_000 });
    26	    let stdout = '';
    27	    let stderr = '';
    28	    child.stdout.on('data', (d) => (stdout += d));
    29	    child.stderr.on('data', (d) => (stderr += d));
    30	    child.on('error', reject);
    31	    child.on('close', (code) => {
    32	      if (code === 0) return resolve({ stdout, stderr });
    33	      const e = Object.assign(new Error(`Command failed: ${cmd}`), { code, stdout, stderr });
    34	      reject(e);
    35	    });
    36	    if (typeof opts.input === 'string' && child.stdin) child.stdin.write(opts.input);
    37	    child.stdin?.end();
    38	  });
    39	}
    40	
    41	// Hook-error diagnostics (gate-2 minor 16 / plan §Transcript policy log
    42	// contract): metadata-only entries — never the hook payload — with bounded
    43	// retention (last 50 entries / 7 days). The log rides .handoff/ so the purge
    44	// walk covers it.
    45	const HOOK_LOG_MAX = 50;
    46	const HOOK_LOG_MAX_AGE_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    47	
    48	/** @param {any} io @param {string} event @param {any} err */
    49	function logHookError(io, event, err) {
    50	  try {
    51	    // The diagnostics log rides .handoff/, so it passes the same managed-tree
    52	    // jail as every other write (threat model): refuse to create or write
    53	    // through a symlinked/escaped .handoff. Diagnostics must never be the hole
    54	    // the jail closes elsewhere — and this stays fail-open (skip, don't throw).
    55	    if (!checkHandoffTree(io.cwd, io).ok) return;
    56	    const dir = `${io.cwd}/.handoff/log`;
    57	    io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
    58	    const path = `${dir}/hook-errors.jsonl`;
    59	    const nowIso = typeof io.now === 'function' ? io.now() : new Date().toISOString();
    60	    /** @type {any[]} */
    61	    let entries = [];
    62	    try {
    63	      entries = io.fs
    64	        .readFileSync(path, 'utf8')
    65	        .split('\n')
    66	        .filter((/** @type {string} */ l) => l.trim() !== '')
    67	        .map((/** @type {string} */ l) => {
    68	          try {
    69	            return JSON.parse(l);
    70	          } catch {
    71	            return null;
    72	          }
    73	        })
    74	        .filter(Boolean);
    75	    } catch {
    76	      entries = [];
    77	    }
    78	    entries.push({ ts: nowIso, event, code: err?.code ?? null });
    79	    const cutoff = Date.parse(nowIso) - HOOK_LOG_MAX_AGE_MS;
    80	    entries = entries.filter((e) => typeof e.ts === 'string' && Date.parse(e.ts) >= cutoff).slice(-HOOK_LOG_MAX);
    81	    io.fs.writeFileSync(path, entries.map((e) => JSON.stringify(e)).join('\n') + '\n');
    82	  } catch {
    83	    // diagnostics must never break fail-open
    84	  }
    85	}
    86	
    87	/**
    88	 * Run a core baton command as a child process, forwarding the raw hook payload
    89	 * as the child's stdin. Rejections are swallowed (fail-open) — core commands
    90	 * carry their own hook-safety, and Claude ignores hook exit codes anyway —
    91	 * but each failure leaves a metadata-only diagnostic in .handoff/log/.
    92	 * @param {any} io @param {string[]} argv @param {string} event
    93	 */
    94	async function baton(io, argv, event) {
    95	  try {
    96	    await io.execFile('node', [batonBin(io), ...argv], { input: io.stdin });
    97	  } catch (err) {
    98	    // fail-open: a failing child must not break the session
    99	    logHookError(io, event, err);
   100	  }
   101	}
   102	
   103	/** @param {any} io @returns {any | null} parsed hook payload, or null */
   104	function parsePayload(io) {
   105	  try {
   106	    return JSON.parse(typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '');
   107	  } catch {
   108	    return null;
   109	  }
   110	}
   111	
   112	/**
   113	 * SessionStart: not a checkpoint. If a pending bundle from ANOTHER platform
   114	 * exists (sealed, or open-but-limit-hit — the unsealed limit death), inject a
   115	 * receive suggestion via the Claude context shape. Own-platform bundles are
   116	 * this session's own work; quiet no-op.
   117	 * @param {any} io
   118	 */
   119	function sessionStart(io) {
   120	  // The bundle is untrusted input (threat model): refuse a symlinked/escaped
   121	  // managed tree before reading, and never interpolate a raw bundle field into
   122	  // the injected context (iter-3 F12) — a hostile committed .handoff/bundle.json
   123	  // could otherwise steer session context with an arbitrary origin string.
   124	  if (!checkHandoffTree(io.cwd, io).ok) return 0;
   125	  let bundle;
   126	  try {
   127	    bundle = JSON.parse(io.fs.readFileSync(`${io.cwd}/.handoff/bundle.json`, 'utf8'));
   128	  } catch {
   129	    return 0;
   130	  }
   131	  const origin = bundle?.origin?.platform;
   132	  const pending = bundle?.handoff?.status === 'sealed' || bundle?.handoff?.reasonClass === 'usage-limit';
   133	  const foreign = typeof origin === 'string' && origin !== 'claude-code';
   134	  if (pending && foreign) {
   135	    // Allowlist the origin label — never echo the untrusted string verbatim.
   136	    const label = origin === 'codex' || origin === 'cursor' ? origin : 'another platform';
   137	    io.stdout.write(
   138	      JSON.stringify({
   139	        hookSpecificOutput: {
   140	          hookEventName: 'SessionStart',
   141	          additionalContext: `A handoff bundle from ${label} is pending in .handoff/ (${bundle.handoff.status === 'sealed' ? 'sealed' : 'limit-hit before sealing'}). Suggest running /baton:receive to resume that task with remapped roles.`,
   142	        },
   143	      }) + '\n',
   144	    );
   145	  }
   146	  return 0;
   147	}
   148	
   149	/**
   150	 * @param {string[]} args argv after the script path; args[0] is the hook event
   151	 * @param {any} io injected {cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now, …}
   152	 * @returns {Promise<number>} always 0 — fail-open by construction
   153	 */
   154	export async function runHook(args, io) {
   155	  try {
   156	    const event = args[0] ?? 'unknown';
   157	
   158	    if (event === 'SessionStart') return sessionStart(io);
   159	
   160	    if (event === 'StopFailure') {
   161	      const errorType = parsePayload(io)?.error?.type;
   162	      const detectArgs = ['detect', '--platform', 'claude-code'];
   163	      if (typeof errorType === 'string') detectArgs.push('--structured-error-type', errorType);
   164	      await baton(io, detectArgs, event);
   165	      await baton(io, ['checkpoint', '--platform', 'claude-code'], event);
   166	      return 0;
   167	    }
   168	
   169	    // Stop / PreCompact / SessionEnd — and any future event — are routine
   170	    // mechanical checkpoints; core normalize degrades unknown payloads safely.
   171	    await baton(io, ['checkpoint', '--platform', 'claude-code'], event);
   172	    return 0;
   173	  } catch {
   174	    return 0;
   175	  }
   176	}
   177	
   178	// Main entry: hooks.json executes this file directly (`node …/hook.mjs <Event>`),
   179	// so a real invocation must build the real io and run — an export alone is a
   180	// production no-op (gate-2 reviewer-b finding 1).
   181	const isMain = (() => {
   182	  try {
   183	    return process.argv[1] ? import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(process.argv[1]).href : false;
   184	  } catch {
   185	    return false;
   186	  }
   187	})();
   188	
   189	if (isMain) {
   190	  let stdin = '';
   191	  try {
   192	    if (!process.stdin.isTTY) stdin = readFileSync(0, 'utf8');
   193	  } catch {
   194	    stdin = '';
   195	  }
   196	  const io = {
   197	    cwd: process.cwd(),
   198	    env: process.env,
   199	    stdin,
   200	    stdout: process.stdout,
   201	    stderr: process.stderr,
   202	    fs: nodeFs,
   203	    execFile: execFileWithInput,
   204	    now: () => new Date().toISOString(),
   205	  };
   206	  runHook(process.argv.slice(2), io).then(
   207	    (code) => process.exit(code),
   208	    () => process.exit(0),
   209	  );
   210	}
     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { makeIo } from '../helpers/fakeio.mjs';
     4	import { bundlePaths } from '../../core/src/bundle/store.mjs';
     5	// SOLE RED CAUSE for this file: the module below does not exist yet, so this
     6	// static import throws ERR_MODULE_NOT_FOUND at load and every test in the file
     7	// reds with that single, unambiguous cause. (core/* and helper imports above all
     8	// resolve — only hook.mjs is missing.)
     9	import { runHook } from '../../adapters/claude-code/scripts/hook.mjs';
    10	
    11	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12	// ADAPTERS wave — RED. Behavioural contract for the Claude Code single hook
    13	// entrypoint, exercised over injected io (mirrors the core command tests:
    14	// cmdX(args, io) -> exit code). The adapter CALLS the core CLI — these tests pin
    15	// the adapter SURFACE only (event→command mapping, the SessionStart context
    16	// shape, fail-open), never core checkpoint/detect logic.
    17	//
    18	// TARGET MODULE: adapters/claude-code/scripts/hook.mjs (sole target).
    19	//
    20	// Source of truth: plan §Claude Code adapter hook table + platform-notes
    21	// §Claude Code (SessionStart injects hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext).
    22	//
    23	// PINNED CONTRACT (open details resolved here):
    24	//   S0. SIGNATURE. `runHook(args, io) => Promise<number>`, where args[0] is the
    25	//       hook event name (passed by hooks.json) and io is the injected object
    26	//       {cwd, env, stdin, stdout, stderr, fs, execFile, now, …}. The hook payload
    27	//       arrives as the STRING io.stdin (Claude writes hook JSON on stdin).
    28	//   S1. BATON REACH. To run a core command, hook.mjs invokes the core CLI as a
    29	//       child process via io.execFile: it resolves the baton bin as
    30	//       `${io.env.CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT}/core/bin/baton.mjs` and calls
    31	//       io.execFile(node, [batonBin, <subcmd>, …flags], {input}). Tests assert on
    32	//       the recorded invocation. (Verifier fold V1) EVERY core child call —
    33	//       checkpoint AND detect — must use that resolved bin path and must forward
    34	//       the raw hook payload as the child's stdin: opts.input === io.stdin. An
    35	//       adapter that bypasses the core CLI or drops the payload cannot pass.
    36	//   S2. MAPPING. Stop / PreCompact / SessionEnd -> exactly one `checkpoint
    37	//       --platform claude-code` invocation, identifying the source event.
    38	//       StopFailure -> BOTH a `detect --platform claude-code` invocation (the
    39	//       classify step) AND a `checkpoint --platform claude-code` invocation
    40	//       stamped with a stop-failure trigger; a rate_limit StopFailure carries the
    41	//       rate_limit signal through to the checkpoint (marks the bundle limit-hit).
    42	//   S3. SessionStart is NOT a checkpoint: it spawns NO checkpoint child. Instead
    43	//       it reads <cwd>/.handoff/bundle.json (read-only, via io.fs) and, when a
    44	//       PENDING (sealed OR usage-limit) bundle FROM ANOTHER PLATFORM exists, emits
    45	//       exactly the Claude context shape on stdout:
    46	//         {"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"SessionStart",
    47	//                                  "additionalContext":"…suggest /baton:receive…"}}
    48	//       (Verifier fold V2) The full pending matrix is pinned:
    49	//         positive:  foreign SEALED bundle            -> context
    50	//         positive:  foreign OPEN + reasonClass usage-limit (limit death before
    51	//                    finalize — the primary failure case) -> context
    52	//         negative:  foreign OPEN non-limit bundle    -> no context
    53	//         negative:  own-platform open non-limit      -> no context
    54	//         negative:  no bundle at all                 -> no context
    55	//       proving BOTH disjuncts of "sealed OR limit-hit" AND "foreign only".
    56	//   S4. FAIL-OPEN. Every error path (unparseable stdin, a rejected core
    57	//       invocation, a missing/corrupt bundle) returns 0 and never throws — a hook
    58	//       must never break the host session. Claude ignores the exit code anyway.
    59	//
    60	// "limit-hit" (S2/S3): pinned to the documented handoff fields — a bundle whose
    61	// handoff.status==='sealed' OR handoff.reasonClass==='usage-limit' is "pending".
    62	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63	
    64	const T0 = '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z';
    65	const paths = bundlePaths('/repo');
    66	const PLUGIN_ROOT = '/repo';
    67	
    68	/**
    69	 * Build an io whose execFile RECORDS every invocation. `respond` lets a test
    70	 * control the child's result (resolve/reject); default resolves empty.
    71	 */
    72	function makeHookIo({ stdin = '', files = {}, respond } = {}) {
    73	  const io = makeIo({ env: { CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT: PLUGIN_ROOT }, stdin, files, now: T0 });
    74	  const calls = [];
    75	  io.execFile = (cmd, args = [], opts = {}) => {
    76	    calls.push({ cmd, args: Array.isArray(args) ? args : [], opts: opts || {} });
    77	    if (respond) return respond({ cmd, args, opts });
    78	    return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
    79	  };
    80	  return { io, calls };
    81	}
    82	
    83	const line = (c) => [c.cmd, ...c.args].join(' ');
    84	const inputOf = (c) => (typeof c.opts.input === 'string' ? c.opts.input : '');
    85	const serialize = (c) => `${line(c)} ${inputOf(c)}`;
    86	const findSub = (calls, sub) => calls.filter((c) => c.args.includes(sub) || new RegExp(`\\b${sub}\\b`).test(line(c)));
    87	
    88	const BATON_BIN = `${PLUGIN_ROOT}/core/bin/baton.mjs`;
    89	/** (V1) Every core child call goes through the core CLI with the payload forwarded. */
    90	function assertBatonCall(c, io, label) {
    91	  assert.ok(line(c).includes(BATON_BIN), `${label}: the invocation uses the baton bin resolved under CLAUDE_PLUGIN_ROOT (${BATON_BIN}); got: ${line(c)}`);
    92	  assert.equal(inputOf(c), io.stdin, `${label}: the raw hook payload is forwarded as the child's stdin (opts.input === io.stdin)`);
    93	}
    94	
    95	function claudeBundle(overrides = {}) {
    96	  return {
    97	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
    98	    bundleId: 'b_hook0000000000',
    99	    generation: 1,
   100	    createdAt: T0,
   101	    updatedAt: T0,
   102	    origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: 'sess-cc', unstable: false },
   103	    task: { goal: 'g', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
   104	    plan: { steps: [] },
   105	    decisions: [],
   106	    files: { touched: [] },
   107	    roles: { assignments: {} },
   108	    git: null,
   109	    handoff: { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
   110	    journalSeq: 0,
   111	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
   112	    dedupeRing: [],
   113	    ...overrides,
   114	  };
   115	}
   116	const snapText = (b) => JSON.stringify(b, null, 2) + '\n';
   117	const payload = (event, extra = {}) => JSON.stringify({ hook_event_name: event, session_id: 'sess-cc', cwd: '/repo', ...extra });
   118	
   119	// ===========================================================================
   120	describe('hook.mjs — routine checkpoint events map to a single core checkpoint (S1, S2)', () => {
   121	  for (const event of ['Stop', 'PreCompact', 'SessionEnd']) {
   122	    it(`${event} -> one \`checkpoint --platform claude-code\` reaching core/bin/baton.mjs`, async () => {
   123	      const { io, calls } = makeHookIo({ stdin: payload(event), files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(claudeBundle()) } });
   124	
   125	      const code = await runHook([event], io);
   126	      assert.equal(code, 0, 'a checkpoint hook always exits 0 (fail-open by construction)');
   127	
   128	      const ckpts = findSub(calls, 'checkpoint');
   129	      assert.equal(ckpts.length, 1, `${event} maps to exactly one checkpoint invocation`);
   130	      const c = ckpts[0];
   131	      assertBatonCall(c, io, `${event} checkpoint`);
   132	      assert.ok(c.args.includes('--platform') && c.args.includes('claude-code'), 'checkpoint is stamped --platform claude-code');
   133	      assert.match(serialize(c), new RegExp(event.replace('Session', 'session.?').toLowerCase(), 'i'), `the invocation identifies the ${event} source`);
   134	
   135	      assert.equal(findSub(calls, 'detect').length, 0, `${event} is a routine checkpoint, not a detect`);
   136	      // (V1) No core call anywhere in this event bypassed the baton bin.
   137	      for (const any of calls) assertBatonCall(any, io, `${event} core call`);
   138	    });
   139	  }
   140	});
   141	
   142	// ===========================================================================
   143	describe('hook.mjs — StopFailure classifies AND checkpoints (S2)', () => {
   144	  it('a rate_limit StopFailure invokes detect and a stop-failure checkpoint carrying the rate_limit signal', async () => {
   145	    const { io, calls } = makeHookIo({
   146	      stdin: payload('StopFailure', { error: { type: 'rate_limit' } }),
   147	      files: { [paths.snapshot]: snapText(claudeBundle()) },
   148	      // Mirror real `baton detect`: usage-limit exits 10 with a JSON verdict on
   149	      // stdout. hook.mjs must tolerate the nonzero exit (fail-open) and still route.
   150	      respond: ({ args }) => {
   151	        if (args.includes('detect')) {
   152	          const e = new Error('Command failed');
   153	          e.code = 10;
   154	          e.stdout = JSON.stringify({ ok: true, data: { class: 'usage-limit' }, warnings: [], error: null }) + '\n';
   155	          e.stderr = '';
   156	          return Promise.reject(e);
   157	        }
   158	        return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   159	      },
   160	    });
   161	
   162	    const code = await runHook(['StopFailure'], io);
   163	    assert.equal(code, 0, 'StopFailure handling is fail-open even when detect exits nonzero');
   164	
   165	    const detects = findSub(calls, 'detect');
   166	    assert.equal(detects.length, 1, 'StopFailure runs the payload through `baton detect` (the classify step)');
   167	    assertBatonCall(detects[0], io, 'StopFailure detect');
   168	    assert.ok(detects[0].args.includes('--platform') && detects[0].args.includes('claude-code'), 'detect is stamped --platform claude-code');
   169	
   170	    const ckpts = findSub(calls, 'checkpoint');
   171	    assert.equal(ckpts.length, 1, 'StopFailure also writes a mechanical checkpoint');
   172	    const c = ckpts[0];
   173	    assertBatonCall(c, io, 'StopFailure checkpoint');
   174	    assert.ok(c.args.includes('--platform') && c.args.includes('claude-code'), 'checkpoint is stamped --platform claude-code');
   175	    assert.match(serialize(c), /stop-?failure/i, 'the checkpoint is stamped with a stop-failure trigger');
   176	    assert.match(serialize(c), /rate_limit/i, 'the rate_limit signal flows through to the checkpoint (marks the bundle limit-hit)');
   177	
   178	    // (V1) No core call anywhere in this event bypassed the baton bin.
   179	    for (const any of calls) assertBatonCall(any, io, 'StopFailure core call');
   180	  });
   181	});

   186	    const bundle = claudeBundle({
   187	      origin: { platform: 'codex', model: 'gpt-5.6-sol', sessionHint: 'sess-x', unstable: false },

   200	    assert.ok(typeof ctx === 'string' && ctx.length > 0, 'additionalContext is a non-empty string');

   202	  });
   203	
   204	  it('(V2 positive) an OPEN foreign bundle marked usage-limit (limit death BEFORE finalize) emits additionalContext', async () => {

   210	    });

    75	  section(
    40	/**
    41	 * Structural validation, deep (gate-2 fix 6): bundles are untrusted input, so
    42	 * every nested shape that render or the commands consume is checked — a string
    43	 * task.constraints must fail here, not crash renderHandoffMd — and file paths
    44	 * are jailed to the repository. Accumulates one {path, msg} per problem;
    45	 * unknown extra fields are tolerated everywhere (forward compatibility).
    46	 * @param {any} obj
    47	 * @returns {{ok: boolean, errors: {path: string, msg: string}[]}}
    48	 */
    49	export function validateBundle(obj) {
    50	  /** @type {{path: string, msg: string}[]} */
    51	  const errors = [];
    52	  const err = (/** @type {string} */ path, /** @type {string} */ msg) => errors.push({ path, msg });
    53	
    54	  if (obj === null || typeof obj !== 'object') {
    55	    return { ok: false, errors: [{ path: '', msg: 'bundle must be an object' }] };
    56	  }
    57	
    58	  if (obj.schema !== BUNDLE_SCHEMA) err('schema', `expected "${BUNDLE_SCHEMA}", got ${JSON.stringify(obj.schema)}`);
    59	  if (typeof obj.bundleId !== 'string') err('bundleId', 'must be a string');
    60	  if (typeof obj.generation !== 'number') err('generation', 'must be a number');
    61	  if (typeof obj.createdAt !== 'string') err('createdAt', 'must be an ISO string');
    62	  if (typeof obj.updatedAt !== 'string') err('updatedAt', 'must be an ISO string');
    63	  if (typeof obj.journalSeq !== 'number') err('journalSeq', 'must be a number');
    64	  if (!Array.isArray(obj.decisions)) {
    65	    err('decisions', 'must be an array');
    66	  } else {
    67	    obj.decisions.forEach((/** @type {any} */ d, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
    68	      if (d === null || typeof d !== 'object') err(`decisions[${i}]`, 'must be an object');
    69	    });
    70	  }
    71	  if (!Array.isArray(obj.dedupeRing)) err('dedupeRing', 'must be an array');
    72	
    73	  if (obj.origin === null || typeof obj.origin !== 'object') {
    74	    err('origin', 'must be an object');
    75	  } else if (typeof obj.origin.platform !== 'string') {
    76	    err('origin.platform', 'must be a string');
    77	  }
    78	
    79	  if (obj.task === null || typeof obj.task !== 'object') {
    80	    err('task', 'must be an object');
    81	  } else {
    82	    if (typeof obj.task.goal !== 'string') err('task.goal', 'must be a string');
    83	    if (!Array.isArray(obj.task.constraints)) {
    84	      err('task.constraints', 'must be an array of strings');
    85	    } else {
    86	      obj.task.constraints.forEach((/** @type {any} */ c, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
    87	        if (typeof c !== 'string') err(`task.constraints[${i}]`, 'must be a string');
    88	      });
    89	    }
    90	    if (!Array.isArray(obj.task.acceptance)) err('task.acceptance', 'must be an array');
    91	  }
    92	
    93	  if (obj.plan === null || typeof obj.plan !== 'object') {
    94	    err('plan', 'must be an object');
    95	  } else if (!Array.isArray(obj.plan.steps)) {
    96	    err('plan.steps', 'must be an array');
    97	  } else {
    98	    obj.plan.steps.forEach((/** @type {any} */ s, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
    99	      if (s === null || typeof s !== 'object') err(`plan.steps[${i}]`, 'must be an object');
   100	    });
   101	  }
   102	
   103	  if (obj.files === null || typeof obj.files !== 'object') {
   104	    err('files', 'must be an object');
   105	  } else if (!Array.isArray(obj.files.touched)) {
   106	    err('files.touched', 'must be an array');
   107	  } else {
   108	    obj.files.touched.forEach((/** @type {any} */ f, /** @type {number} */ i) => {
   109	      if (f === null || typeof f !== 'object') err(`files.touched[${i}]`, 'must be an object');
   110	      else if (jailRelPath(f.path) === null) {
   111	        err(`files.touched[${i}].path`, 'must be a normalized repo-relative path (absolute and escaping paths are refused)');
   112	      }
   113	    });
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  if (obj.roles === null || typeof obj.roles !== 'object') {
   117	    err('roles', 'must be an object');
   118	  } else if (obj.roles.assignments === null || typeof obj.roles.assignments !== 'object' || Array.isArray(obj.roles.assignments)) {
   119	    err('roles.assignments', 'must be an object');
   120	  } else {
   121	    // Each assignment value is consumed by renderHandoffMd (reads .platform,
   122	    // .model, .mode) — a null/non-object value passed validation and crashed
   123	    // render (gate-2 iter-2 B4). Every value must be a plain object.
   124	    for (const [role, a] of Object.entries(obj.roles.assignments)) {
   125	      if (a === null || typeof a !== 'object' || Array.isArray(a)) err(`roles.assignments.${role}`, 'must be an object');
   126	    }
   127	  }
   128	  if (obj.compaction === null || typeof obj.compaction !== 'object') err('compaction', 'must be an object');
   129	
   130	  if (obj.handoff === null || typeof obj.handoff !== 'object') {
   131	    err('handoff', 'must be an object');
   132	  } else {
   133	    if (!HANDOFF_STATUSES.includes(obj.handoff.status)) {
   134	      err('handoff.status', `must be one of ${HANDOFF_STATUSES.join('|')}`);
   135	    }
   136	    if (obj.handoff.receive_log !== undefined && !Array.isArray(obj.handoff.receive_log)) {
   137	      err('handoff.receive_log', 'must be an array');
   138	    }
   139	  }
   140	
   141	  // git is nullable by design; when present it must be an object.
   142	  if (obj.git !== null && obj.git !== undefined && typeof obj.git !== 'object') err('git', 'must be an object or null');
   143	
   144	  return { ok: errors.length === 0, errors };
   145	}

   147	/**
   148	 * Version migration seam. Schema v1 is identity; anything else is unsupported.
   149	 * @param {any} obj
   150	 */
   151	export function migrate(obj) {
   152	  if (obj?.schema === BUNDLE_SCHEMA) return structuredClone(obj);
   153	  throw new Error(`unsupported bundle schema ${JSON.stringify(obj?.schema)}; this baton understands ${BUNDLE_SCHEMA}`);
   154	}

     5	import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
     6	import { join } from 'node:path';
     7	import { cmdDoctor } from '../../core/src/commands/doctor.mjs';
     8	
     9	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    10	// Gate-2 iteration-3 F4 (reviewer-a #3): doctor writes .handoff/log/
    11	// probe-cache.json — a managed-tree write that must pass the jail, so a
    12	// symlinked .handoff/log cannot redirect the cache outside the repository.
    13	// Real fs (the memfs fake cannot represent symlinks). execFile is stubbed so no
    14	// real harness binary is invoked.
    15	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    16	
    17	/** @type {string[]} */
    18	const dirs = [];
    19	const scratch = (prefix) => {
    20	  const d = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), prefix));
    21	  dirs.push(d);
    22	  return d;
    23	};
    24	after(() => {
    25	  for (const d of dirs) rmSync(d, { recursive: true, force: true });
    26	});
    27	
    28	const doctorIo = (root) => {
    29	  let out = '';
    30	  return {
    31	    cwd: root,
    32	    env: {},
    33	    fs: nodeFs,
    34	    now: () => '2026-07-12T00:00:00.000Z',
    35	    // Every child invocation (version/probe/df/git) returns benign empty output.
    36	    execFile: () => Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }),
    37	    stdout: { write: (/** @type {string} */ s) => ((out += s), true) },
    38	    stderr: { write: () => true },
    39	    get output() {
    40	      return out;
    41	    },
    42	  };
    43	};
    44	
    45	describe('doctor probe-cache write honors the managed-tree jail (F4)', () => {
    46	  it('does NOT write the probe cache through a symlinked .handoff/log', async () => {
    47	    const root = scratch('baton-doctorcache-');
    48	    const outside = scratch('baton-doctorcache-outside-');
    49	    mkdirSync(join(root, '.handoff'), { recursive: true });
    50	    // Give doctor a repo marker so resolveRoot stays at root.
    51	    writeFileSync(join(root, 'baton.config.json'), '{"schema":"baton/config@1","roles":{},"platforms":{},"defaults":{}}');
    52	    symlinkSync(outside, join(root, '.handoff', 'log'), 'dir');
    53	
    54	    const io = doctorIo(root);
    55	    const code = await cmdDoctor(['--json'], io);
    56	    assert.equal(typeof code, 'number', 'doctor returns an exit code, does not throw');
    57	
    58	    // Nothing was written through the link into the outside directory.
    59	    assert.deepEqual(readdirSync(outside), [], 'no probe-cache.json escaped through the symlinked log dir');
    60	    assert.ok(nodeFs.lstatSync(join(root, '.handoff', 'log')).isSymbolicLink(), 'the log symlink is left intact');
    61	  });
    62	
    63	  it('DOES write the probe cache when the tree is safe (guard is symlink-specific)', async () => {
    64	    const root = scratch('baton-doctorcache-ok-');
    65	    mkdirSync(join(root, '.handoff'), { recursive: true });
    66	    writeFileSync(join(root, 'baton.config.json'), '{"schema":"baton/config@1","roles":{},"platforms":{},"defaults":{}}');
    67	
    68	    const io = doctorIo(root);
    69	    await cmdDoctor(['--json'], io);
    70	    assert.ok(existsSync(join(root, '.handoff', 'log', 'probe-cache.json')), 'the cache is written on a safe tree');
    71	  });
    72	});
     1	/**
     2	 * Filesystem primitives with crash-safety discipline.
     3	 * All functions take an injected fs-like object so tests run on a memfs fake.
     4	 */
     5	
     6	let tmpCounter = 0;
     7	
     8	/** @param {string} path */
     9	function tmpPathFor(path) {
    10	  tmpCounter += 1;
    11	  return `${path}.tmp.${process.pid}-${tmpCounter}`;
    12	}
    13	
    14	/**
    15	 * Rename with retry on EPERM (Windows AV/indexer lock pattern): 3 attempts total.
    16	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} from @param {string} to
    17	 */
    18	function renameWithRetry(fs, from, to) {
    19	  let lastErr;
    20	  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 3; attempt += 1) {
    21	    try {
    22	      fs.renameSync(from, to);
    23	      return;
    24	    } catch (err) {
    25	      lastErr = err;
    26	      if (!err || /** @type {any} */ (err).code !== 'EPERM') throw err;
    27	    }
    28	  }
    29	  throw lastErr;
    30	}
    31	
    32	/**
    33	 * Write text atomically: tmp sibling + rename. The live target is never
    34	 * truncated or partially written.
    35	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {string} content
    36	 */
    37	export function atomicWriteText(fs, path, content) {
    38	  const tmp = tmpPathFor(path);
    39	  fs.writeFileSync(tmp, content);
    40	  try {
    41	    renameWithRetry(fs, tmp, path);
    42	  } catch (err) {
    43	    try {
    44	      fs.unlinkSync(tmp);
    45	    } catch {
    46	      // leftover tmp is harmless; cleanupTmp removes it later
    47	    }
    48	    throw err;
    49	  }
    50	}
    51	
    52	/**
    53	 * Atomically write pretty-printed JSON (2-space, trailing newline).
    54	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {unknown} obj
    55	 */
    56	export function atomicWriteJson(fs, path, obj) {
    57	  atomicWriteText(fs, path, JSON.stringify(obj, null, 2) + '\n');
    58	}
    59	
    60	/**
    61	 * Read + parse JSON without ever throwing.
    62	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path
    63	 * @returns {{ok: true, value: any} | {ok: false, error: string}}
    64	 */
    65	export function safeReadJson(fs, path) {
    66	  let raw;
    67	  try {
    68	    raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    69	  } catch (err) {
    70	    return { ok: false, error: `read failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.code ?? err}` };
    71	  }
    72	  try {
    73	    return { ok: true, value: JSON.parse(raw) };
    74	  } catch (err) {
    75	    return { ok: false, error: `parse failed: ${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? err}` };
    76	  }
    77	}
    78	
    79	/**
    80	 * Copy the current content to <path>.bak (when present), then write atomically.
    81	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {string} content
    82	 */
    83	export function backupThenWrite(fs, path, content) {
    84	  if (fs.existsSync(path)) {
    85	    fs.copyFileSync(path, `${path}.bak`);
    86	  }
    87	  atomicWriteText(fs, path, content);
    88	}
    89	
    90	/**
    91	 * Remove leftover *.tmp.* files in a directory (crash debris).
    92	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} dir
    93	 */
    94	export function cleanupTmp(fs, dir) {
    95	  if (!fs.existsSync(dir)) return;
    96	  for (const name of fs.readdirSync(dir)) {
    97	    if (/\.tmp\./.test(name)) {
    98	      try {
    99	        fs.unlinkSync(`${dir}/${name}`);
   100	      } catch {
   101	        // already gone or locked; not fatal
   102	      }
   103	    }
   104	  }
   105	}
   106	
   107	/**
   108	 * @param {any} fs @param {string} path
   109	 */
   110	export function ensureDir(fs, path) {
   111	  fs.mkdirSync(path, { recursive: true });
   112	}

     1	import { safeReadJson } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
     2	
     3	// Doctor's probe results live in .handoff/log/probe-cache.json for 15 minutes
     4	// (plan §Availability probing). This reader is the consumption side (gate-2
     5	// major 13): remap's resolver and receive's token derivation take the FRESH
     6	// cache; a stale or absent cache reads as null — offline resolution stays
     7	// possible, flagged degraded by the resolver.
     8	
     9	export const PROBE_CACHE_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
    10	
    11	/**
    12	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
    13	 * @returns {Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}
    14	 */
    15	export function readProbeCache(root, io) {
    16	  const cached = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${root}/.handoff/log/probe-cache.json`);
    17	  if (!cached.ok || typeof cached.value?.at !== 'string' || !Array.isArray(cached.value.records)) return null;
    18	  if (!(Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(cached.value.at) < PROBE_CACHE_MS)) return null;
    19	  /** @type {Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}>} */
    20	  const out = {};
    21	  for (const r of cached.value.records) {
    22	    // capability null in a probe record means the binary was not found — a
    23	    // VERIFIED not-installed, which the resolver must skip, not select.
    24	    if (r && typeof r.platform === 'string') out[r.platform] = { capability: r.capability ?? 'not-installed', outcome: r.outcome ?? 'error' };
    25	  }
    26	  return out;
    27	}

     1	import { loadConfig } from '../roles/matrix.mjs';
     2	import { resolveRoles } from '../roles/resolve.mjs';
     3	import { readProbeCache } from '../roles/availability.mjs';
     4	import { emitEnvelope, parseFlags, resolveRoot, usageError } from './shared.mjs';
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * `baton remap` — resolve the committed role matrix for a destination platform.
     8	 * Read-only: prints assignments, never mutates the bundle (receive owns that).
     9	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    10	 * @returns {number}
    11	 */
    12	export function cmdRemap(args, io) {
    13	  const { flags } = parseFlags(args);
    14	  const to = typeof flags.to === 'string' ? flags.to : null;
    15	  if (!to) return usageError(io, flags, 'remap', '--to <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
    16	
    17	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    18	  const { config, errors } = loadConfig(root, io);
    19	  if (!config) {
    20	    const msg = errors.map((e) => (e.path ? `${e.path}: ${e.msg}` : e.msg)).join('; ');
    21	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-config', msg } });
    22	    else io.stderr.write(`baton remap: ${msg}\n`);
    23	    return 1;
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  const avoid = typeof flags.avoid === 'string' ? flags.avoid.split(',') : [];
    27	  const { assignments, notes } = resolveRoles({
    28	    config,
    29	    to,
    30	    avoid,
    31	    nativeOnly: flags['native-only'] === true,
    32	    // The fresh doctor probe cache feeds eligibility (gate-2 major 13):
    33	    // rate-limited platforms are skipped like avoid[] entries; a stale or
    34	    // absent cache resolves offline with degraded flags.
    35	    probes: readProbeCache(root, io),
    36	  });
    37	
    38	  if (flags.json) {
    39	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { assignments, notes } });
    40	  } else {
    41	    for (const [role, a] of Object.entries(assignments)) {
    42	      io.stdout.write(`${role}: ${a.platform ?? '—'}/${a.model ?? '—'}${a.effort ? `@${a.effort}` : ''} (${a.mode})\n`);
    43	    }
    44	  }
    45	  return 0;
    46	}

     6	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     7	// Gate-2 iteration-2 findings B1 + B2 (reviewer-a #3, #4): the dead-lock
     8	// takeover must atomically RE-CLAIM the lock dir (not just republish owner.json
     9	// into the existing one), and every lock/recover mutation must pass the
    10	// managed-tree jail first so a symlinked .handoff/lock cannot be followed
    11	// outside the repository.
    12	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13	
    14	const OWNER = '/repo/.handoff/lock/owner.json';
    15	const deadOwner = JSON.stringify({ host: 'host-A', pid: 999, startTime: 5, fencingToken: 'STALE', acquiredAt: 'x', heartbeatAt: 'x' });
    16	
    17	describe('dead-takeover re-claims the lock dir atomically (B1)', () => {
    18	  it('takeover renames the stale lock dir ASIDE then claims a fresh one (never in-place rm)', () => {
    19	    // Only pid 4242 (this io) is alive → pid 999 is provably dead. The reclaim
    20	    // must be arbitrated by an atomic rename of the SOURCE lock dir, not an
    21	    // rm-then-mkdir that two contenders could both perform (iter-3 finding-1).
    22	    // We trace lock-dir ops in order: the CLAIM must be rename→(rm aside)→mkdir,
    23	    // with no in-place rm of the live /lock before the fresh mkdir. (The final
    24	    // rm of /lock at release, AFTER the mkdir, is correct and expected.)
    25	    const io = makeIo({ files: { [OWNER]: deadOwner } });
    26	    const LOCK = '/repo/.handoff/lock';
    27	    /** @type {string[]} */
    28	    const trace = [];
    29	    const realRename = io.fs.renameSync.bind(io.fs);
    30	    const realRm = io.fs.rmSync.bind(io.fs);
    31	    const realMkdir = io.fs.mkdirSync.bind(io.fs);
    32	    io.fs.renameSync = (from, to) => {
    33	      if (String(from) === LOCK) trace.push(`rename:${/\/lock\.reclaim-/.test(String(to)) ? 'aside' : 'other'}`);
    34	      return realRename(from, to);
    35	    };
    36	    io.fs.rmSync = (p, opts) => {
    37	      if (String(p) === LOCK) trace.push('rm:lock');
    38	      else if (/\/lock\.reclaim-/.test(String(p))) trace.push('rm:aside');
    39	      return realRm(p, opts);
    40	    };
    41	    io.fs.mkdirSync = (p, opts) => {
    42	      if (String(p) === LOCK && !(opts && opts.recursive)) trace.push('mkdir:lock');
    43	      return realMkdir(p, opts);
    44	    };
    45	    const token = withLock('/repo', io, (t) => t);
    46	
    47	    // Trace shape: mkdir:lock (initial EEXIST probe) → rename:aside → rm:aside →
    48	    // mkdir:lock (the winning claim) → rm:lock (release, AFTER the claim).
    49	    const claimMkdir = trace.lastIndexOf('mkdir:lock');
    50	    const renameAt = trace.indexOf('rename:aside');
    51	    const rmAsideAt = trace.indexOf('rm:aside');
    52	    assert.ok(renameAt >= 0, `the stale lock dir is moved aside by an atomic rename; trace ${JSON.stringify(trace)}`);
    53	    assert.ok(renameAt < rmAsideAt && rmAsideAt < claimMkdir, 'the claim order is rename-aside → rm-aside → fresh mkdir');
    54	    const beforeClaim = trace.slice(0, claimMkdir);
    55	    assert.equal(beforeClaim.includes('rm:lock'), false, 'the live lock dir is never rm-ed in place during the claim (only renamed aside)');
    56	    assert.ok(typeof token === 'string' && token.length > 0, 'a fresh token is published after the atomic claim');
    57	  });
    58	
    59	  it('a contender that loses the rename sees ENOENT-as-contention, not a second takeover', () => {
    60	    // Simulate the rename losing the race: the stale dir is already gone when we
    61	    // try to move it aside → LockHeldError (retryable), never a duplicate claim.
    62	    const io = makeIo({ files: { [OWNER]: deadOwner }, lockRetry: { attempts: 0, delayMs: 0 } });
    63	    const realRename = io.fs.renameSync.bind(io.fs);
    64	    io.fs.renameSync = (from, to) => {
    65	      if (String(from).endsWith('/lock') && /\/lock\.reclaim-/.test(String(to))) {
    66	        throw Object.assign(new Error('ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' });
    67	      }
    68	      return realRename(from, to);
    69	    };
    70	    assert.throws(() => withLock('/repo', io, (t) => t), /reclaiming the dead lock|transient contention/i);
    71	  });
    72	
    73	  it('the takeover note is journaled while the lock dir is HELD — seq allocated under the lock (iter-3 F2)', () => {
    74	    // Deterministic white-box guard for the under-lock-collision finding: the
    75	    // old code journaled the takeover during arbitration (dir renamed aside, NOT
    76	    // held), so its seq could collide with a free-path competitor's concurrent
    77	    // append. Assert the note is appended only while the canonical lock dir

Gate 2 iteration 4 remains blocked. The targeted suite passed 116/116 and typecheck is clean; all reviewed commits have the required sole author. However, four prior findings remain incomplete, with additional lock and path-helper regressions.

The verified-closed iteration-3 findings are #1, #2, #3, #5, #8, #10, and #11. F12, originating from reviewer B, is also correctly fixed.

VERDICT: BLOCKED
DISPOSITIONS: 7 of 11 iteration-3 findings verified closed; NOT closed: #4 (F5), #6 (F1), #7 (F10), #9 (F7)
FINDINGS:
1. [blocking] — core/src/bundle/lock.mjs:297-312 — `recoverLock` decides that the owner is dead and then recursively removes the canonical lock path; a contender can atomically replace the stale directory with a live lock between those operations, which recovery then deletes, allowing concurrent mutation — plan §Concurrency atomic lock acquisition — Fix: arbitrate recovery through the same atomic rename-and-fresh-mkdir mechanism as normal dead-lock takeover; never `rmSync` the canonical path based on an earlier inspection.
2. [major] — core/src/bundle/lock.mjs:179-189 — takeover journaling occurs after publishing the new owner but before `acquire` returns its token; if `journalNote` throws, `withLock` never enters its release `finally`, leaving the newly published lock behind — plan §Concurrency; iteration-3 finding #1/F2 — Fix: make the audit append genuinely best-effort after publication, returning the token even when it fails, or explicitly release the published lock before rethrowing.
3. [major] — core/src/commands/checkpoint.mjs:290-297 — when the git refresh returns null, the code leaves `merged.git` unchanged; a reproduction retained `oldsha`, and no degradation warning is emitted — plan §Root discovery & degraded modes; iteration-3 finding #4/F5 — Fix: replace the prior git snapshot with an explicit unavailable/null state and emit a warning whenever refresh fails.
4. [major] — core/src/roles/resolve.mjs:34-41 — degradation still ignores probe outcome: `{capability:"authenticated", outcome:"error"}` and `{capability:"reachable", outcome:"timeout"}` are selected without `degraded`, silently presenting failed reachability as verified healthy — plan §Role matrix availability probing; iteration-3 finding #6/F1 — Fix: mark a selection degraded whenever any required dimension is unverified or the non-rate-limited outcome is not `ok`, while retaining the intended `{installed,ok}` selectable/degraded behavior.
5. [major] — core/src/commands/doctor.mjs:81-105 — doctor still collapses installed, enabled, trusted, and observed-executing into one prose check; trusted is only mentioned as possible guidance and enabled is not represented independently — plan §Codex adapter trust gate; iteration-3 finding #6/F1 — Fix: report four explicit per-hook state fields, including `unknown` where trust cannot be verified, and gate fidelity on the observed state.
6. [major] — core/src/receive/txn.mjs:231-242 — the new open generation spreads the received seal and changes only `status`, retaining an earlier `reasonClass`, reason, and finalized metadata; a normal sealed usage-limit receive therefore causes the next receive to trust the previous generation’s class against the newly adopted origin — plan §Concurrency fresh writable generation and §Limit detector; iteration-3 finding #7/F10 — Fix: clear `reason`, `reasonClass`, `finalizedAt`, and `toPlatformHint` when constructing the fresh open generation.
7. [major] — core/src/detect/probe.mjs:21-22,66-68 — the supposedly non-matching tail remains fixed at U+0001; the exact prior repro, twelve `.*` atoms followed by `\x01`, is still declared safe in about 21 ms, while a 20-character nonmatching input took about 4.5 s — plan §Limit detector hardening; iteration-3 finding #9/F7 — Fix: structurally reject overlapping chained quantified atoms, with the fixed-tail pattern as a regression test; deriving fills from literals does not guarantee a nonmatching tail.
8. [minor] — core/src/util/pathnorm.mjs:38-41 — `isContained("/", "/transcript.jsonl")` returns false because it tests a `//` prefix, so the shared helper refuses valid containment for a repository rooted at `/` — plan §Root discovery, §Transcript policy; changed surface from iteration-3 findings #5/#10 — Fix: handle volume roots explicitly when constructing the containment prefix and add POSIX-root coverage.
